# Diagnosed and fixed a crash in build_autoident_kable_en and build_language_kable_en where municipality #89 had an id_muni absent from auto_data, causing dplyr::filter to return a 0-row tibble. The sapply over numeric(0) columns returned a list instead of a vector, making People/pop_2024 fail with "non-numeric argument to binary operator". Added nrow(row)==0 and nrow(pop_row)==0 early return guards to all four demographic table functions (both kable and wikitable variants).

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01E4M6buSDYtRwfDN4TULQAh"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\n# Create a mapping of OEP 2026 province names to id_prov codes\noep2026_mapping <- tibble:...[truncated, total 728 chars]","intent":"Add province_oep2026 column with the provided OEP 2026 province names mapped to correct id_prov codes"},"intent":"Add province_oep2026 column with the provided OEP 2026 province names mapped to correct id_prov codes","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\n# Create a mapping of OEP 2026 province names to id_prov codes\noep2026_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n  ~id_prov, ~province_oep2026,\n  \"0211\", \"Sud Yungas\",\n  \"0502\", \"Bustillo\",\n  \"0505\", \"Chárcas\",\n  \"0508\", \"Sud Chichas\",\n  \"0509\", \"Nor Lipez\",\n  \"0510\", \"Sud Lipez\",\n  \"0604\", \"Avilés\",\n  \"0606\", \"O' Connor\",\n  \"0703\", \"José Miguel De Velasco\",\n  \"0708\", \"Vallegrande\",\n  \"0712\", \"Obispo Santistéban\",\n  \"0802\", \"Vaca Díez\"\n)\n\n# Add the new column to prov_id_lookup_table\nprov_id_lookup_table <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n  left_join(oep2026_mapping, by = \"id_prov\")\n\n# View relevant rows to verify the mapping\nprov_id_lookup_table |>\n  filter(!is.na(province_oep2026)) |>\n  select(id_prov, province_cpv2024, province_oep2026)\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

## Assistant

I'll create a script that expands the province lookup table with the OEP 2026 names:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01JkVeV4RSPTSnwZLfNYBRam"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","content":"# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 province names\n#\n# This script adds a new colu...[truncated, total 1231 chars]"},"caption":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","captionLink":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 province names\n#\n# This script adds a new column (province_oep2026) to prov_id_lookup_table\n# that maps province ID codes to their OEP (Órgano Electoral Plurinacional)\n# 2026 official names. These may differ from CPV 2024 census names.\n#\n# Sources: OEP 2026 electoral administrative divisions\n\nlibrary(tidyverse)\n\n# Load source helpers\nsource(\"cel_helpers.R\")\n\n# Create mapping of id_prov to OEP 2026 province names\noep2026_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n  ~id_prov, ~province_oep2026,\n  \"0211\", \"Sud Yungas\",\n  \"0502\", \"Bustillo\",\n  \"0505\", \"Chárcas\",\n  \"0508\", \"Sud Chichas\",\n  \"0509\", \"Nor Lipez\",\n  \"0510\", \"Sud Lipez\",\n  \"0604\", \"Avilés\",\n  \"0606\", \"O' Connor\",\n  \"0703\", \"José Miguel De Velasco\",\n  \"0708\", \"Vallegrande\",\n  \"0712\", \"Obispo Santistéban\",\n  \"0802\", \"Vaca Díez\"\n)\n\n# Add the new column to prov_id_lookup_table via left join\nprov_id_lookup_table <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n  left_join(oep2026_mapping, by = \"id_prov\")\n\n# Verification: show rows with OEP 2026 names\nif (interactive()) {\n  cat(\"OEP 2026 province mappings:\\n\")\n  prov_id_lookup_table |>\n    filter(!is.na(province_oep2026)) |>\n    select(id_prov, province_cpv2024, province_oep2026) |>\n    print(n = Inf)\n}\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01JkVeV4RSPTSnwZLfNYBRam"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"Created file: src/expand-prov-lookup.R"},"elapsedMs":1833}</script></tool-result></div>

Done. The script is now in `src/expand-prov-lookup.R`. It:

1. **Documents the purpose** — adds OEP 2026 names to the lookup table
2. **Creates the mapping** — tibble with id_prov codes and their corresponding OEP 2026 names
3. **Joins to the main table** — left join ensures all existing rows are preserved, with NA values where no OEP 2026 name exists
4. **Includes verification** — prints a check showing all 12 mapped provinces in interactive mode

You can source it with `source("src/expand-prov-lookup.R")` to apply the expansion to `prov_id_lookup_table` in your session.

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01QmgzweZjtsVFjNYznT9tof"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 1.4KB, 46 lines, showing lines 1-46]\n     1→# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP ...[truncated, total 1803 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 1.4KB, 46 lines, showing lines 1-46]\n# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 province names\n#\n# This script adds a new column (province_oep2026) to prov_id_lookup_table\n# that maps province ID codes to their OEP (Órgano Electoral Plurinacional)\n# 2026 official names. These may differ from CPV 2024 census names.\n#\n# Sources: OEP 2026 electoral administrative divisions\n\nlibrary(tidyverse)\n\n# Create mapping of id_prov to OEP 2026 province names\noep2026_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n  ~id_prov, ~province_oep2026,\n  \"0211\", \"Sud Yungas\",\n  \"0502\", \"Bustillo\",\n  \"0505\", \"Chárcas\",\n  \"0508\", \"Sud Chichas\",\n  \"0509\", \"Nor Lipez\",\n  \"0510\", \"Sud Lipez\",\n  \"0604\", \"Avilés\",\n  \"0606\", \"O' Connor\",\n  \"0703\", \"José Miguel De Velasco\",\n  \"0708\", \"Vallegrande\",\n  \"0710\", \"Obispo Santistéban\",\n  \"0802\", \"Vaca Díez\"\n)\n\n# Add the new column to prov_id_lookup_table via left join\nif (!\"province_oep2026\" %in% colnames(prov_id_lookup_table)) {\n  prov_id_lookup_table <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n    left_join(oep2026_mapping, by = \"id_prov\")\n} else {\n  warning(\"Column 'province_oep2026' already exists in prov_id_lookup_table. Skipping join.\")\n}\n\n# Verification: show rows with OEP 2026 names\nif (interactive()) {\n  cat(\"OEP 2026 province mappings:\\n\")\n  prov_id_lookup_table |>\n    filter(!is.na(province_oep2026)) |>\n    select(id_prov, province_cpv2024, province_oep2026) |>\n    print(n = Inf)\n}\n\n# saveRDS(prov_id_lookup_table, here::here(\"data\", \"prov_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\n"},"elapsedMs":76}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01U7eXw8TGEiKNRdsiRS4x8H"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","old_string":"# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 province names\n#\n# This script adds a new colu...[truncated, total 1436 chars]","new_string":"# Expand prov_id_lookup_table and muni_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 names\n#\n# This script...[truncated, total 2999 chars]"},"caption":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","captionLink":"src/expand-prov-lookup.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -1,7 +1,7 @@\n-# Expand prov_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 province names\n+# Expand prov_id_lookup_table and muni_id_lookup_table with OEP 2026 names\n #\n-# This script adds a new column (province_oep2026) to prov_id_lookup_table\n-# that maps province ID codes to their OEP (Órgano Electoral Plurinacional)\n+# This script adds new columns to prov_id_lookup_table and muni_id_lookup_table\n+# that map province/municipality ID codes to their OEP (Órgano Electoral Plurinacional)\n # 2026 official names. These may differ from CPV 2024 census names.\n #\n # Sources: OEP 2026 electoral administrative divisions\n@@ -9,7 +9,7 @@\n library(tidyverse)\n \n # Create mapping of id_prov to OEP 2026 province names\n-oep2026_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n+oep2026_prov_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n   ~id_prov, ~province_oep2026,\n   \"0211\", \"Sud Yungas\",\n   \"0502\", \"Bustillo\",\n@@ -21,19 +21,19 @@\n   \"0606\", \"O' Connor\",\n   \"0703\", \"José Miguel De Velasco\",\n   \"0708\", \"Vallegrande\",\n-  \"0710\", \"Obispo Santistéban\",\n+  \"0712\", \"Obispo Santistéban\",\n   \"0802\", \"Vaca Díez\"\n )\n \n # Add the new column to prov_id_lookup_table via left join\n if (!\"province_oep2026\" %in% colnames(prov_id_lookup_table)) {\n   prov_id_lookup_table <- prov_id_lookup_table |>\n-    left_join(oep2026_mapping, by = \"id_prov\")\n+    left_join(oep2026_prov_mapping, by = \"id_prov\")\n } else {\n   warning(\"Column 'province_oep2026' already exists in prov_id_lookup_table. Skipping join.\")\n }\n \n-# Verification: show rows with OEP 2026 names\n+# Verification: show rows with OEP 2026 province names\n if (interactive()) {\n   cat(\"OEP 2026 province mappings:\\n\")\n   prov_id_lookup_table |>\n@@ -42,4 +42,47 @@\n     print(n = Inf)\n }\n \n+# Create mapping of id_muni to OEP 2026 municipality names\n+oep2026_muni_mapping <- tibble::tribble(\n+  ~id_muni, ~muni_oep2026,\n+  \"010201\", \"Villa Azurduy\",\n+  \"010301\", \"Villa Zudañez\",\n+  \"010303\", \"Villa Mojocoya\",\n+  \"010304\", \"Villa Ricardo Mujia-Icla\",\n+  \"010404\", \"Villa Alcalá\",\n+  \"010901\", \"Camataqui - Villa Abecia\",\n+  \"011001\", \"Villa Vaca Guzmán - Muyupampa\",\n+  \"020306\", \"Waldo Ballivian\",\n+  \"030301\", \"Ayopaya (Villa De Independencia)\",\n+  \"030902\", \"Sipe Sipe\",\n+  \"031403\", \"San Benito (Villa José Quintín Mendoza)\",\n+  \"070401\", \"San Pedro De Buena Vista\",\n+  \"050901\", \"Colcha \\\"K\\\"\",\n+  \"051001\", \"San Pablo De Lípez\",\n+  \"051102\", \"Caiza \\\"D\\\"\",\n+  \"060401\", \"Uriondo (Concepción)\",\n+  \"070301\", \"San Ignacio De Velasco\",\n+  \"070302\", \"San Miguel De Velasco\",\n+  \"070902\", \"Pampa Grande\",\n+  \"071002\", \"General Agustín Saavedra\"\n+)\n+\n+# Add the new column to muni_id_lookup_table via left join\n+if (!\"muni_oep2026\" %in% colnames(muni_id_lookup_table)) {\n+  muni_id_lookup_table <- muni_id_lookup_table |>\n+    left_join(oep2026_muni_mapping, by = \"id_muni\")\n+} else {\n+  warning(\"Column 'muni_oep2026' already exists in muni_id_lookup_table. Skipping join.\")\n+}\n+\n+# Verification: show rows with OEP 2026 municipality names\n+if (interactive()) {\n+  cat(\"\\nOEP 2026 municipality mappings:\\n\")\n+  muni_id_lookup_table |>\n+    filter(!is.na(muni_oep2026)) |>\n+    select(id_muni, muni_cpv2024, muni_oep2026, department) |>\n+    print(n = Inf)\n+}\n+\n # saveRDS(prov_id_lookup_table, here::here(\"data\", \"prov_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\n+# saveRDS(muni_id_lookup_table, here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Perfect! The script now includes both province and municipality OEP 2026 mappings. Both lookup tables have been successfully updated with:

- **`prov_id_lookup_table`**: Added `province_oep2026` column with 12 province names
- **`muni_id_lookup_table`**: Added `muni_oep2026` column with 20 municipality names

The script follows the same structure as before:
1. Creates a mapping tibble with ID codes and OEP 2026 names
2. Left joins to the respective lookup tables (preserving all rows)
3. Shows verification output when run interactively

Perfect! Both columns are now fully populated with no NA values. The script now:

1. Creates the OEP 2026 mappings for the 12 provinces and 20 municipalities
2. Joins them to the lookup tables
3. **Fills in all remaining NA values** using `coalesce()` to use the CPV 2024 names where OEP 2026 names aren't defined
4. Verifies that there are zero NA values in either column

So `province_oep2026` and `muni_oep2026` now have complete coverage: the 12/20 special electoral names, with all remaining entries copied from their respective CPV 2024 columns.

CANONICAL SIGLA LOOKUP\n# ==============================================================================\n# Strategy: fingerprint each raw sigla (strip hyphens, spaces, newlines, lowercase),\n# then pick the cleanest form per fingerprint group as the canonical value.\n\nmeta_cols <- c(\"autoridad\", \"validos\", \"blancos\", \"nulos\", \"emitidos\",\n               \"habilitados\", \"participacion\")\n\nfingerprint <- function(x) {\n  x |> str_to_lower() |> str_remove_all(\"[-\\\\s\\n]\")\n}\n\nmeta_fp <- fingerprint(meta_cols)\n\nsigla_lookup <- muni_votes |>\n  map(colnames) |>\n  unlist() |>\n  unique() |>\n  (\\(x) tibble(raw = x))() |>\n  mutate(\n    fp            = fingerprint(raw),\n    has_nl        = str_detect(raw, \"\\n\"),\n    is_upper      = str_detect(raw, \"^[A-Z]\"),\n    has_space_hyp = str_detect(raw, \" - \")   # prefer \"APB-SÚMATE\" over \"APB - SÚMATE\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(!fp %in% meta_fp) |>\n  group_by(fp) |>\n  arrange(has_nl, desc(is_upper), has_space_hyp, raw) |>\n  slice(1) |>\n  ungroup() |>\n  select(fp, canonical = raw)\n\nnormalize_sigla <- function(x) {\n  fp  <- fingerprint(x)\n  idx <- match(fp, sigla_lookup$fp)\n  can <- sigla_lookup$canonical[idx]\n  fallback <- str_squish(str_replace_all(str_replace_all(x, \"-\\n\", \"-\"), \"\\n\", \" \"))\n  result <- if_else(!is.na(can), can, fallback)\n  # Final pass: clean residual newlines (e.g. when canonical was itself the only form)\n  str_squish(str_replace_all(str_replace_all(result, \"-\\n\", \"-\"), \"\\n\", \" \"))\n}\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# 2. CLEAN DATA OBJECTS\n# ==============================================================================\n\n# muni_votes: clean column names (party siglas)\nmuni_votes_clean <- muni_votes |>\n  map(~ { colnames(.x) <- normalize_sigla(colnames(.x)); .x })\n\n# concejales: filter artifact rows, normalize silla and sigla\nconcejales <- as_tibble(concejales_df) |>\n  filter(silla != \"N.º\") |>\n  mutate(\n    silla  = as.integer(str_remove(silla, \"\\\\.$\")),\n    sigla  = normalize_sigla(sigla),\n    nombre = str_squish(nombre)\n  )\n\n# alcaldes: normalize sigla and nombre\nalcaldes <- as_tibble(alcaldes_df) |>\n  mutate(\n    sigla  = normalize_sigla(sigla),\n    nombre = str_squish(nombre)\n  )\n\nsource(here::here(\"R\", \"id-for-muni-prov.R\"))\n\n# muni_roster: plain tibble\nmuni_roster <- as_tibble(muni_roster_df) |>\n  mutate(num = row_number())\n\nmuni_roster_1 <- muni_roster |>\n  filter(province==\"CERCADO\") |>\n  filter(municipality != \"SAN JAVIER\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = id_for_municipality_vec(municipality)) |>\n  mutate(id_dep = str_sub(id_muni, 1L, 2L)) |>\n  left_join(dep_id_lookup_table) |>\n  select(-id_dep) |>\n  mutate(department = toupper(department) ) |>\n  relocate(id_muni) |>\n  relocate(department, .after=province)\n\nmuni_roster_2 <- muni_roster |>\n  filter(province==\"CERCADO\") |>\n  filter(municipality == \"SAN JAVIER\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = \"080102\",\n         department = \"BENI\") |>\n  relocate(id_muni) |>\n  relocate(department, .after=province)\n\nmuni_roster_3 <- muni_roster |>\n  filter(province!=\"CERCADO\") |>\n  mutate(id_prov = id_for_province_vec(province)) |>\n  mutate(id_dep = str_sub(id_prov, 1L, 2L)) |>\n  left_join(dep_id_lookup_table) |>\n  select(-id_dep, -id_prov) |>\n  mutate(department = toupper(department) ) |>\n  mutate(id_muni = id_for_municipality_vec(municipality, department)) |>\n  relocate(id_muni) |>\n  relocate(department, .after=province)\n\nmuni_roster <- bind_rows(muni_roster_1, muni_roster_2, muni_roster_3) |>\n  arrange(num)\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# 3. COUNCIL SEAT SEQUENCE AUDIT\n# ==============================================================================\n# For each municipality, check whether titular and suplente seats form a clean\n# unbroken sequence 1..n_sillas with no duplicates.\n\nconcejal_summary <- concejales |>\n  group_by(municipio, provincia) |>\n  summarise(\n    n_sillas       = max(silla, na.rm = TRUE),\n    n_titular      = sum(tipo == \"TITULAR\"),\n    n_suplente     = sum(tipo == \"SUPLENTE\"),\n    titular_clean  = {\n      seats <- silla[tipo == \"TITULAR\"]\n      setequal(seats, seq_len(max(silla, na.rm = },\n    .groups = \"drop\"\n  ) |>\n  arrange(provincia, municipio)\n\n# Quick breakdown of problem types\nconcejal_summary |>\n  filter(!titular_clean | !suplente_clean) |>\n  mutate(\n    issue = tbl_mat <- do.call(rbind, lapply(tbl, function(row) {\n    sapply(row, function(cell)\n      if (is.null(cell) || trimws(as.character(cell)) == \"\") NA else as.character(cell))\n  }))\n\n  temp_df <- as.data.frame(tbl_mat, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n  if (nrow(temp_df) <= 1 || ncol(temp_df) < 4) return(NULL)\n\n  data_rows <- temp_df[2:nrow(temp_df), 1:4]\n  colnames(data_rows) <- c(\"silla\", \"tipo\", \"nombre\", \"sigla\")\n\n  # Detect one-column right-shift: silla col all-NA but next col has integers\n  if (ncol(temp_df) >= 5 &&\n      all(is.na(data_rows$silla)) &&\n clean_data <- data_rows |>\n    fill(silla, sigla, .direction = \"down\") |>\n    filter(!is.na(silla)) |>\n    mutate(nombre = str_replace_all(nombre, \"\\n\", \" \"))\n\n  if (nrow(clean_data) == 0) = clean_data$sigla,\n    stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n  )\n}\n\n# Process one municipality (index i) from a page.\n# anchors: list with top_anchors, bottom_anchors, left_anchors, column_top_anchors\n# words_df: word-level extract for the page (used for IOC detection)\n# Returns a list: muni_name, prov_name, vote_df, alcalde_row, concejal_rows, ioc_rows\nprocess_municipality <- function(page, i, anchors, words_df) {\n  top_anchors        <- anchors$top_anchors\n  bottom_anchors     <- anchors$bottom_anchors\n  left_anchors       <- anchors$left_anchors\n  column_top_anchors <- anchors$column_top_anchors\n\n  # --- Vote table (full-width header band) ---\n  dynamic_box <- c(0, top_anchors[i] - 5, page$width, bottom_anchors[i] - 5)\n  table_data  <- page$crop(dynamic_box)$extract_table()\n\n  if (is.null(table_data) || length(table_data) == 0)\n    stop(\"Top vote table missing or unreadable.\")\n\n  tbl_matrix <- do.call(rbind, lapply(table_data, function(row) {\n    sapply(row, function(cell) if (is.null(cell)) NA else as.character(cell))\n  }))\n\n  header_text <- paste(tbl_matrix[1, ], collapse = \" \")\n  muni_name   <- \"UNKNOWN_MUNI\"\n  if (is.na(prov_name)) prov_name <- \"UNKNOWN_PROV\"\n\n  headers <- tbl_matrix[2, ]\n  vote_df  <- as.data.frame(tbl_matrix[3:4, ], stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n  colnames(vote_df) <- headers\n\n  n_cols <- ncol(vote_df)\n  if (n_cols >= 7) {\n    cols_to_clean <- c(1, (n_cols - 5):n_cols)\n    colnames(vote_df)[cols_to_clean] <- colnames(vote_df)[cols_to_clean] |>\n      str_to_lower() |>\n      stri_trans_general(\"Latin-ASCII\")\n  }\n\n  # --- Section boundaries (truncate above IOC table if present) ---\n  true_section_bottom <- if (i < length(top_anchors)) top_anchors[i + 1] - 5 else page$height\n  section_bottom      <- true_section_bottom\n\n  indigenous_table_y <- words_df |>\n    filter(text == \"NACIONES\", top > bottom_anchors[i], top < section_bottom) |>\n    pull(top)\n  has_ioc <- length(indigenous_table_y) > 0\n  if (has_ioc) section_bottom <- min(indigenous_table_y) - 5\n\n  # --- Box coordinates ---\n  l_anch2 <- as.numeric(left_anchors[i, 2]) - 5\n  l_anch3 <- as.numeric(left_anchors[i, 3]) - 5\n  c_top1  <- as.numeric(column_top_anchors[i, 1]) - 8\n  c_top2  <- as.numeric(column_top_anchors[i, 2]) - 8\n  c_top3  <- as.numeric(column_top_anchors[i, 3]) - 8\n\n  mayor_box <- c(0,           bottom_anchors[i] - 3, l_anch2 - 5, c_top1)\n  left_box  <- c(0,           c_top1,                l_anch2 - 2, section_bottom)\n  mid_box   <- c(l_anch2 - 3, c_top2,                l_anch3 - 2, section_bottom)\n  right_box <- c(l_anch3 - 5, c_top3,                page$width,  section_bottom)\n\n  # --- Alcalde ---\n  mayor_tables <- page$crop(mayor_box)$extract_tables()\n  if (length(mayor_tables) == 0) stop(\"Mayor table missing entirely.\")\n\n  alcalde_matrix <- do.call(rbind, lapply(mayor_tables[[1]], function(row) {\n    sapply(row, function(cell) if (is.null(cell)) NA else as.character(cell))\n  }))\n\n  alcalde_row <- data.frame(\n    municipio = muni_name,\n    provincia = prov_name,\n    autoridad = if (nrow(alcalde_matrix) >= 1) alcalde_matrix[1, 1] else NA,\n    nombre    = if (nrow(alcalde_matrix) >= 3) alcalde_matrix[3, 1] else NA,\n    sigla     = if (nrow(alcalde_matrix) >= 3 && ncol(alcalde_matrix) >= 2) alcalde_matrix[3, 2] else NA,\n    stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n  )\n\n  # --- Concejales (three columns) ---\n  left_tables  <- page$crop(left_box)$extract_tables()\n  mid_tables   <- page$crop(mid_box)$extract_tables()\n  right_tables <- page$crop(right_box)$extract_tables()\n\n  council_raw_list <- list()\n  if (length(left_tables)  >= 1) council_raw_list[[length(council_raw_list) + 1]] <- left_tables[[1]]\n  if (length(mid_tables)   >= 1) council_raw_list[[length(council_raw_list) + 1]] <- mid_tables[[1]]\n  if (length(right_tables) >= 1) council_raw_list[[length(council_raw_list) + 1]] <- right_tables[[1]]\n\n  concejal_rows <- do.call(rbind, lapply(\n    council_raw_list, parse_council_table,\n    muni_name = muni_name, prov_name = prov_name\n  ))\n\n  # --- IOC concejales ---\n  ioc_rows <- NULL\n  if (has_ioc) {\n    ioc_box    <- c(0, min(indigenous_table_y) - 5, page$width, true_section_bottom)\n    ioc_tables <- page$crop(ioc_box)$extract_tables()\n\n    if (length(ioc_tables) > 0) {\n      ioc_mat <- do.call(rbind, lapply(ioc_tables[[1]], function(row) {\n        sapply(row, function(cell)\n          if (is.null(cell) || trimws(as.character(cell)) == \"\") NA else as.character(cell))\n      }))\n\n      ioc_df_temp <- as.data.frame(ioc_mat, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n\n      if (nrow(ioc_df_temp) > 1) {\n        while (ncol(ioc_df_temp) < 4) ioc_df_temp <- cbind(ioc_df_temp, NA)\n\n        ioc_data_rows <- ioc_df_temp[2:nrow(ioc_df_temp), 1:4]\n        colnames(ioc_data_rows) <- c(\"silla\", \"pueblo\", \"tipo\", \"nombre\")\n\n        clean_ioc <- ioc_data_rows |>\n          filter(!grepl(\"TIT./SUP.\", tipo, ignore.case = TRUE)) |>\n          fill(silla, pueblo, .direction = \"down\") |>\n          filter(!is.na(silla))\n\n        ioc_rows_list <- list()\n        for (r in seq_len(nrow(clean_ioc))) {\n          r_silla      <- clean_ioc$silla[r]\n          r_pueblo     <- str_replace_all(clean_ioc$pueblo[r], \"\\n\", \" \")\n          r_tipo       <- clean_ioc$tipo[r]\n          r_nombre     <- clean_ioc$nombre[r]\n          is_pendiente <- grepl(\"PENDIENTE\", paste(r_tipo, r_nombre), ignore.case = TRUE)\n\n          if (is_pendiente) {\n            ioc_rows_list[[length(ioc_rows_list) + 1]] <- data.frame(\n              municipio = muni_name, provincia = prov_name, autoridad = \"CONCEJO IOC\",\n              pueblo = r_pueblo, silla = r_silla, tipo = \"TITULAR\",\n              nombre = NA, pendiente = TRUE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n            )\n            ioc_rows_list[[length(ioc_rows_list) + 1]] <- data.frame(\n              municipio = muni_name, provincia = prov_name, autoridad = \"CONCEJO IOC\",\n              pueblo = r_pueblo, silla = r_silla, tipo = \"SUPLENTE\",\n              nombre = NA, pendiente = TRUE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n            )\n          } else if (!is.na(r_tipo) && !is.na(r_nombre)) {\n            ioc_rows_list[[length(ioc_rows_list) + 1]] <- data.frame(\n              municipio = muni_name, provincia = prov_name, autoridad = \"CONCEJO IOC\",\n              pueblo = r_pueblo, silla = r_silla, tipo = r_tipo,\n              nombre = str_replace_all(r_nombre, \"\\n\", \" \"), pendiente = FALSE,\n              stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n            )\n          }\n        }\n        ioc_rows <- do.call(rbind, ioc_rows_list)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  list(\n    muni_name     = muni_name,\n    prov_name     = prov_name,\n    vote_df       = vote_df,\n    alcalde_row   = alcalde_row,\n    concejal_rows = concejal_rows,\n    ioc_rows      = ioc_rows,\n    has_ioc       = has_ioc\n  )\n}\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# IMPORT\n# ==============================================================================\n\npdfplumber <- reticulate::import(\"pdfplumber\")\npdf        <- pdfplumber$open(\"data/23-04-2026-part-1-y-2-V6_compressed.pdf\")\n\n# Master data structures\nmuni_roster_df     <- data.frame(municipality = character(), province = character(),\n                                page = integer(), stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\nmuni_votes       <- list()\nalcaldes_df      <- data.frame(municipio = character(), provincia = character(),\n sigla = character(), stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\nmuni_council_ioc <- data.frame(municipio = character(), provincia = character(),\n                                autoridad = character(), pueblo = stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\nerror_log        <- data.frame(page = integer(), municipality_index = integer(),\n                                error_message = character(), stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n\ntotal_pages <- length(pdf$pages)\n\nfor (page_num in 24:total_pages - 1) {\n  message(sprintf(\"Processing page %d of %d...\", page_num, total_pages))\n\n  page <- page:\", e$message), stringsAsFactors = FALSE))\n    NULL\n  })\n  if (is.null(page)) next\n\n  words_df <- tryCatch({\n    map_dfr(page$extract_words(), ~ as.data.frame(.x))\n  }, error = function(e) data.frame())\n\n  if (nrow(words_df) == 0) {\n    message(sprintf(\"--> Skipping page %d (no text layout detected)\", page_num))\n    next\n  }\n\n  top_anchors         <- words_df |> filter(text == \"MUNICIPIO:\") |> pull(top)\n  bottom_anchors      <- words_df |> filter(text %in% c(\"ALCALDE\", \"ALCALDESA\")) |> pull(top)\n  # Extract N.º anchors, excluding any that align with IOC table headers\n  n_anchors_df <- words_df |> filter(text %in% c(\"N.º\", \"N.°\", \"N°\"))\n  special_y_coords <- words_df |>\n    filter(text %in% c(\"PUEBLO\", \"NACIÓN\", \"PUEBLOS\", \"NACIONES\")) |>\n    pull(top)\n  if (length(special_y_coords) > 0) {\n    valid_rows <- sapply(n_anchors_df$top, function(y) {\n      !any(abs(y - special_y_coords) < 10)\n    })\n    n_anchors_df <- <div><tool-call id="toolu_01NajuWkEJDqbiXZ9VQo2qNb"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"concejal_summary |> filter(n_sillas %% 2 == 0) |>\n  filter(!(municipio %in% ioc_munis)) ->...[truncated, total 303 chars]","new_string":"concejal_summary |> filter(n_sillas %% 2 == 0) |>\n  filter(!(municipio %in% ioc_munis)) ->...[truncated, total 1002 chars]"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -181,4 +181,25 @@\n   print(truly_even_concejo)\n }\n \n+# ==============================================================================\n+# 4. COMBINED COUNCIL TABLE\n+# ==============================================================================\n+# muni_concejo_comb: all concejales plus IOC seats appended as seat n+1.\n+# esp_ioc = TRUE marks the added indigenous seats; FALSE for all regular seats.\n \n+ioc_next_silla <- concejal_summary |>\n+  filter(municipio %in% ioc_munis) |>\n+  select(municipio, n_sillas)\n+\n+muni_concejo_comb <- bind_rows(\n+  concejales |>\n+    mutate(esp_ioc = FALSE),\n+  muni_concejal_ioc |>\n+    left_join(ioc_next_silla, by = \"municipio\") |>\n+    mutate(\n+      silla   = n_sillas + 1L,\n+      esp_ioc = TRUE\n+    ) |>\n+    select(-n_sillas)\n+)\n+\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

COMBINED COUNCIL TABLE\n# ==============================================================================\n# muni_concejo_comb: all concejales plus IOC seats appended as seat n+1.\n# esp_ioc = TRUE marks the added indigenous seats; FALSE for all regular seats.\n\nioc_next_silla <- concejal_summary |>\n  filter(municipio %in% ioc_munis) |>\n  select(municipio, n_sillas)\n\nmuni_concejo_comb <- bind_rows(\n  concejales |>\n    mutate(esp_ioc = FALSE),\n  muni_concejal_ioc |>\n    left_join(ioc_next_silla, by = \"municipio\") |>\n    mutate(\n      silla   = n_sillas + 1L,\n      esp_ioc = TRUE\n    ) |>\n    select(-n_sillas)\n)\n\n"},"elapsedMs":86}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01W33uzRDoLEe3Wjdc9C7fox"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"muni_concejo_comb <- bind_rows(\n  concejales |>\n    mutate(esp_ioc = FALSE),\n  muni_concej...[truncated, total 248 chars]","new_string":"muni_concejo_comb <- bind_rows(\n  concejales |>\n    mutate(esp_ioc = FALSE),\n  muni_concej...[truncated, total 4365 chars]"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -226,3 +226,105 @@\n     select(-n_sillas)\n )\n \n+# ==============================================================================\n+# 5. PARTY SUMMARY FUNCTION\n+# ==============================================================================\n+# Aggregates vote share, elected alcaldes, elected concejal titulares, and\n+# municipalities represented, for a given scope of municipalities.\n+#\n+# Arguments:\n+#   id_munis   - character vector of id_muni codes (takes priority)\n+#   department - character scalar/vector of department names (case-insensitive)\n+# If both NULL, aggregates across all municipalities.\n+#\n+# Returns a tibble with one row per party (sigla), sorted by n_concejales desc.\n+# Depends on: muni_votes_clean, alcaldes, concejales, muni_roster (global env).\n+\n+party_summary <- function(id_munis = NULL, department = NULL) {\n+  # Determine scope\n+  scope <- muni_roster\n+  if (!is.null(id_munis)) {\n+    scope <- scope |> filter(id_muni %in% id_munis)\n+  } else if (!is.null(department)) {\n+    scope <- scope |> filter(toupper(.data$department) %in% toupper(department))\n+  }\n+\n+  muni_names  <- scope$municipality\n+  meta_cols_v <- c(\"autoridad\", \"validos\", \"blancos\", \"nulos\", \"emitidos\",\n+                   \"habilitados\", \"participacion\")\n+\n+  # Parse Bolivian-format numeric strings: \"6.457\" (thousands sep) → 6457\n+  parse_num <- function(x) as.numeric(str_remove_all(as.character(x), \"\\\\.\"))\n+\n+  # Build tidy vote frame: one row per municipality × race × party\n+  votes_tidy <- map_dfr(scope$num, function(idx) {\n+    df         <- muni_votes_clean[[idx]]\n+    party_cols <- setdiff(colnames(df), meta_cols_v)\n+\n+    df |>\n+      select(autoridad, validos, all_of(party_cols)) |>\n+      mutate(\n+        validos = parse_num(validos),\n+        across(all_of(party_cols), parse_num)\n+      ) |>\n+      pivot_longer(all_of(party_cols), names_to = \"sigla\", values_to = \"votes\") |>\n+      mutate(\n+        municipio = scope$municipality[scope$num == idx],\n+        race = case_when(\n+          str_detect(autoridad, regex(\"alcalde\",  ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ \"alcalde\",\n+          str_detect(autoridad, regex(\"concejal\", ignore_case = TRUE)) ~ \"concejal\",\n+          TRUE ~ NA_character_\n+        )\n+      ) |>\n+      filter(!is.na(race))\n+  })\n+\n+  # Total valid votes per race (denominators for share)\n+  validos_by_race <- votes_tidy |>\n+    distinct(municipio, race, validos) |>\n+    group_by(race) |>\n+    summarise(total_validos = sum(validos, na.rm = TRUE), .groups = \"drop\")\n+\n+  alcalde_validos  <- validos_by_race |> filter(race == \"alcalde\")  |> pull(total_validos)\n+  concejal_validos <- validos_by_race |> filter(race == \"concejal\") |> pull(total_validos)\n+\n+  # Party vote totals by race, pivoted wide\n+  vote_totals <- votes_tidy |>\n+    group_by(sigla, race) |>\n+    summarise(total_votes = sum(votes, na.rm = TRUE), .groups = \"drop\") |>\n+    pivot_wider(names_from = race, values_from = total_votes,\n+                names_prefix = \"votes_\",\n+                values_fill  = 0)\n+\n+  # Elected alcaldes per party\n+  mayor_counts <- alcaldes |>\n+    filter(municipio %in% muni_names) |>\n+    count(sigla, name = \"n_alcaldes\")\n+\n+  # Elected concejal titulares per party (excludes IOC seats)\n+  concejal_counts <- concejales |>\n+    filter(municipio %in% muni_names, tipo == \"TITULAR\") |>\n+    count(sigla, name = \"n_concejales\")\n+\n+  # Municipalities where party holds alcalde or ≥1 concejal titular\n+  muni_repr <- bind_rows(\n+    alcaldes  |> filter(municipio %in% muni_names) |> select(sigla, municipio),\n+    concejales |> filter(municipio %in% muni_names, tipo == \"TITULAR\") |> select(sigla, municipio)\n+  ) |>\n+    distinct() |>\n+    count(sigla, name = \"n_munis\")\n+\n+  vote_totals |>\n+    full_join(mayor_counts,    by = \"sigla\") |>\n+    full_join(concejal_counts, by = \"sigla\") |>\n+    full_join(muni_repr,       by = \"sigla\") |>\n+    mutate(\n+      vote_share_alcalde  = votes_alcalde  / alcalde_validos,\n+      vote_share_concejal = votes_concejal / concejal_validos,\n+      across(c(n_alcaldes, n_concejales, n_munis), ~ replace_na(.x, 0L))\n+    ) |>\n+    arrange(desc(n_concejales), desc(vote_share_concejal)) |>\n+    select(sigla, vote_share_concejal, vote_share_alcalde,\n+           n_alcaldes, n_concejales, n_munis)\n+}\n+\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

The issue is that pdfplumber captures these as hyphenated line-break artifacts: `habili-\ntados`, `emiti-dos`, etc. The fix belongs in the `muni_votes_clean` step, right where column names are normalized. I'll add a `dehyphenate` step there that handles both the `\n`-present and `\n`-already-stripped cases:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01LVPEVxyQUKkFJqGv1xAV3a"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"# muni_votes: clean column names (party siglas)\nmuni_votes_clean <- muni_votes |>\n  map(~ ...[truncated, total 144 chars]","new_string":"# Repair PDF line-break hyphenation in column names.\n# pdfplumber splits long header words...[truncated, total 728 chars]"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -76,9 +76,24 @@\n # 2. CLEAN DATA OBJECTS\n # ==============================================================================\n \n-# muni_votes: clean column names (party siglas)\n+# Repair PDF line-break hyphenation in column names.\n+# pdfplumber splits long header words with a soft hyphen, producing either\n+# \"habili-\\ntados\" (newline present) or \"habili-tados\" (newline stripped).\n+# Both cases are handled: first remove \"-\\n\", then fix any remaining literals.\n+dehyphenate <- function(x) {\n+  x |>\n+    str_replace_all(\"-\\n\", \"\") |>\n+    str_replace_all(c(\n+      \"habili-tados\"   = \"habilitados\",\n+      \"emiti-dos\"      = \"emitidos\",\n+      \"blan-cos\"       = \"blancos\",\n+      \"partici-pacion\" = \"participacion\"\n+    ))\n+}\n+\n+# muni_votes: fix hyphenated meta column names, then normalize party siglas\n muni_votes_clean <- muni_votes |>\n-  map(~ { colnames(.x) <- normalize_sigla(colnames(.x)); .x })\n+  map(~ { colnames(.x) <- dehyphenate(colnames(.x)) |> normalize_sigla(); .x })\n \n # concejales: filter artifact rows, normalize silla and sigla\n concejales <- as_tibble(concejales_df) |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01LVPEVxyQUKkFJqGv1xAV3a"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"Updated oep-results-2026-cleaning.R"},"elapsedMs":312}</script></tool-result></div>

The `dehyphenate` function runs before `normalize_sigla`, so by the time the fingerprinting and meta-column filtering happen, the column names are already clean. The two-stage approach (`-\n` first, then literal replacements) ensures both variants from the PDF are caught regardless of whether pdfplumber preserved the newline.

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01QhEiu7E9cCQpTUPLqmiCx9"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 12.1KB, 346 lines, showing lines 38-67 (truncated)]\n    38→meta_cols <- c(\"autorida...[truncated, total 1103 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 12.1KB, 346 lines, showing lines 38-67 (truncated)]\nmeta_cols <- c(\"autoridad\", \"validos\", \"blancos\", \"nulos\", \"emitidos\",\n               \"habilitados\", \"participacion\")\n\nfingerprint <- function(x) {\n  x |> str_to_lower() |> str_remove_all(\"[-\\\\s\\n]\")\n}\n\nmeta_fp <- fingerprint(meta_cols)\n\nsigla_lookup <- muni_votes |>\n  map(colnames) |>\n  unlist() |>\n  unique() |>\n  (\\(x) tibble(raw = x))() |>\n  mutate(\n    fp            = fingerprint(raw),\n    has_nl        = str_detect(raw, \"\\n\"),\n    is_upper      = str_detect(raw, \"^[A-Z]\"),\n    has_space_hyp = str_detect(raw, \" - \")   # prefer \"APB-SÚMATE\" over \"APB - SÚMATE\"\n  ) |>\n  filter(!fp %in% meta_fp) |>\n  group_by(fp) |>\n  arrange(has_nl, desc(is_upper), has_space_hyp, raw) |>\n  slice(1) |>\n  ungroup() |>\n  select(fp, canonical = raw)\n\nnormalize_sigla <- function(x) {\n  fp  <- fingerprint(x)\n  idx <- match(fp, sigla_lookup$fp)"},"elapsedMs":74}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_0117utChgmuEvBeW1QkUdr62"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"normalize_sigla <- function(x) {\n  fp  <- fingerprint(x)\n  idx <- match(fp, sigla_lookup$f...[truncated, total 429 chars]","new_string":"# Manual overrides: raw sigla → correct canonical form.\n# Applied before fingerprint looku...[truncated, total 1525 chars]"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -62,7 +62,30 @@\n   ungroup() |>\n   select(fp, canonical = raw)\n \n+# Manual overrides: raw sigla → correct canonical form.\n+# Applied before fingerprint lookup so corrected forms can still match vote-table\n+# canonicals. Cases arise from PDF extraction artifacts (line-break hyphens,\n+# missing/extra periods) or names that differ between ballot headers and result tables.\n+sigla_overrides <- c(\n+  # Line-break hyphenation in result-table name fields\n+  \"ALIANZA PA-TRIA UNIDOS\"  = \"PATRIA-UNIDOS\",\n+  \"SOLUCIO-NES\"             = \"SOLUCIONES CON TODOS\",\n+  # PDF name differs from vote-table header\n+  \"ALIANZA PATRIA\"          = \"PATRIA-UNIDOS\",\n+  # Period variants (fingerprint strips hyphens/spaces but not periods)\n+  \"AS\"                      = \"A.S.\",\n+  \"AS.\"                     = \"A.S.\",\n+  \"T.S\"                     = \"T.S.\",\n+  \"CONACH - W.\"             = \"CONACH-W\",\n+  # Spelling discrepancy between ballot header and result table\n+  \"JACHAJAKIS-ASOLFERSORC\"  = \"JACHAJAKISASOLFESORC\"\n+)\n+\n normalize_sigla <- function(x) {\n+  # Apply manual overrides before fingerprint lookup\n+  override_idx <- match(x, names(sigla_overrides))\n+  x <- if_else(!is.na(override_idx), sigla_overrides[override_idx], x)\n+\n   fp  <- fingerprint(x)\n   idx <- match(fp, sigla_lookup$fp)\n   can <- sigla_lookup$canonical[idx]\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

We're going to build off of a current script to generate Wikitext material that has been copied to bolivia-muni-generator.qmd. This will generate a web page filled with materials that can be copied to Wikipedia.

Currently, it draws on a targets pipeline, but here we will draw on local data.

Each time we will be building Wikitext for all of the municipalities in Bolivia, which will be sorted by id_muni.  Correct names for Wikipedia purposes are indexed in the columns id_muni (INE code), muni_anexo (municipality name), and department of  muni_id_lookup_table, which can be loaded from an RDS in data/muni_id_lookup_table.rds 

For now, we will focus on building a council table for each municipality based on muni_concejo_comb. 

All of the relevant data for a given municipality is in:
muni_concejo_comb |> filter(id_muni=="010101") |> select(silla:pendiente)

(except sourcing which we'll handle later.)

We'll build a custom version of get_wikitable in src/wikipedia-tools.R called build_council_wikitable(). We need a custom version because we want repeated seat numbers to have a rowspan of 2 so they bridge both the titular and alternate members. 

For now, we'll do this in a Spanish output, matching the input data. Each name must be converted to Title case.

Use planning if this task is too complex to do in one shot.

old-revision)\nget_wikitext_from_url <- function(url) {\n  parsed <- httr::parse_url(url)\n  lang <- str_split(parsed$hostname, \"\\\\.\")[[1]][1]\n\n  if (!is.null(parsed$query$oldid)) {\n    title <- parsed$query$title\n    revid <- parsed$query$oldid\n    return(get_wikitext_by_revid(title, revid, lang = lang))\n  } else {\n    title <- str_replace(parsed$path, \"wiki/\", \"\")\n    return(get_wikitext_by_name(title, lang = lang))\n  }\n}\n\n# ---- Text extraction (from get-wikipedia-text-1.R) ----------------------------\n\n#' Strip wikitext markup and split into sentence-like fragments (≥5 words)\nextract_clean_fragments <- function(wikitext, keep_link_text = FALSE) {\n  clean_text <- str_replace_all(wikitext, \"(?s)<ref.*?>.*?</ref>\", \"\")\n  clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text, \"<ref.*?/>\", \"\")\n  clean_text <- gsub(\"\\\\{\\\\{(?:[^{}]|(?R))*\\\\}\\\\}\", \"\", clean_text, perl = TRUE)\n  clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text, \"(?i)\\\\[\\\\[(File|Image):.*?\\\\]\\\\]\", \"\")\n\n  if (keep_link_text) {\n    clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text,\n      \"\\\\[\\\\[(?:[^|\\\\]]*\\\\|)?([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\", \"\\\\1\")\n  } else {\n    clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text, \"\\\\[\\\\[.*?\\\\]\\\\]\", \"\")\n  }\n\n  clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text, \"==+.*?==+\", \"\")\n  clean_text <- str_replace_all(clean_text, \"''+\", \"\")\n\n  fragments <- unlist(str_split(clean_text, \"[\\\\.\\\\!\\\\?\\\\n\\\\r]\"))\n  fragments <- str_trim(fragments)\n  fragments <- str_replace_all(fragments, \"\\\\s+\", \" \")\n  final_list <- fragments[str_count(fragments, \"\\\\w+\") >= 5]\n  unique(final_list)\n}\n\n# ---- Wikipedia search / match (from add-wikipedia-matches.R) ------------------\n\n#' Search Wikipedia and add match columns to a data frame\nadd_wikipedia_matches <- function(df, name_col = \"name\", lang = \"en\",\n                                  delay = 0.5, limit = 5) {\n  stopifnot(is.data.frame(df), name_col %in% names(df))\n  names_vec <- as.character(df[[name_col]])\n\n  search_one <- function(query_str, lang = \"en\", limit = 5) {\n    if (is.na(query_str) || nzchar(trimws(query_str)) == FALSE) {\n      return(list(found = FALSE, title = NA_character_,\n                  url = NA_character_, snippet = NA_character_))\n    }\n\n    res <- NULL\n    try({\n      res <- WikipediR::page_search(language = lang, project = \"wikipedia\",\n                                    query = query_str, limit = limit)\n    }, silent = TRUE)\n\n    if (is.null(res) || !is.list(res) ||\n        is.null(res$query) || is.null(res$query$search)) {\n      api_url <- paste0(\"https://\", lang, \".wikipedia.org/w/api.php\")\n      resp <- httr::GET(api_url, query = list(\n        action = \"query\", list = \"search\",\n        srsearch = query_str, srlimit = limit, format = \"json\"\n      ))\n      if (httr::status_code(resp) >= 400) {\n        return(list(found = FALSE, title = NA_character_,\n                    url = NA_character_, snippet = NA_character_))\n      }\n      res <- fromJSON(content(resp, as = \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"),\n                      simplifyVector = FALSE)\n    }\n\n    search_hits <- res$query$search %||% res$search\n    if (is.null(search_hits) || length(search_hits) == 0) {\n      return(list(found = FALSE, title = NA_character_,\n                  url = NA_character_, snippet = NA_character_))\n    }\n\n    top <- search_hits[[1]]\n    top_title   <- as.character(top$title %||% NA_character_)\n    top_snippet <- as.character(top$snippet %||% NA_character_)\n    title_url   <- gsub(\" \", \"_\", top_title)\n    url <- paste0(\"https://\", lang, \".wikipedia.org/wiki/\",\n                  utils::URLencode(title_url, reserved = TRUE))\n    list(found = TRUE, title = top_title, url = url, snippet = top_snippet)\n  }\n\n  results <- vector(\"list\", length(names_vec))\n  for (i in seq_along(names_vec)) {\n    if (i > 1 && delay > 0) Sys.sleep(delay)\n    res_i <- tryCatch(\n      search_one(names_vec[i], lang = lang, limit = limit),\n      error = function(e) list(found = FALSE, title = NA_character_,\n                               url = NA_character_, snippet = NA_character_)\n    )\n    matched <- FALSE\n    if (isTRUE(res_i$found) && !is.na(res_i$title)) {\n      norm_q <- tolower(gsub(\"\\\\s+\", \"\", names_vec[i]))\n      norm_t <- tolower(gsub(\"\\\\s+\", \"\", res_i$title))\n      matched <- nzchar(norm_q) && nzchar(norm_t) && identical(norm_q, norm_t)\n    }\n    results[[i]] <- list(\n      wikipedia_found   = isTRUE(res_i$found),\n      wikipedia_match   = isTRUE(matched),\n      wikipedia_title   = if (isTRUE(res_i$found)) res_i$title else NA_character_,\n      wikipedia_url     = if (isTRUE(res_i$found)) res_i$url   else NA_character_,\n      wikipedia_snippet = if (isTRUE(res_i$found)) res_i$snippet else NA_character_\n    )\n  }\n\n  results_df <- bind_rows(lapply(results, as_tibble))\n  out <- bind_cols(df, results_df)\n  out$wikipedia_found <- as.logical(out$wikipedia_found)\n  out$wikipedia_match <- as.logical(out$wikipedia_match)\n  out\n}\n\n# ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n\n#' Format a data frame as a MediaWiki wikitable string\nget_wikitable <- function(df, caption = NULL, class = \"wikitable sortable\",\n                          column_names = NULL, source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n  out <- c()\n  out <- c(out, paste0('{| class=\"', class, '\"'))\n  if (!is.null(caption)) out <- c(out, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n\n  if (is.null(column_names)) column_names <- names(df)\n  out <- c(out, paste0(\"! \", paste(column_names, collapse = \" !! \")))\n\n  formatted_rows <- apply(df, 1, function(row) {\n    row_clean <- gsub(\"\\\\|\", \"|\", as.character(row))\n    row_clean[is.na(row_clean)] <- \"\"\n    paste0(\"| \", paste(row_clean, collapse = \" || \"))\n  })\n\n  # Paste data rows\n  out <- c(out, paste0(\"|-\\n\", formatted_rows))\n\n  # Append Source row if provided\n  if (!is.null(source)) {\n    source_text <- paste0(\"'''Source:''' \", source)\n\n    # Append the reference tag if source_refs is provided\n    if (!is.null(source_refs)) {\n      source_text <- paste0(source_text, \"<ref>\", source_refs, \"</ref>\")\n    }\n\n    # Calculate colspan based on the number of columns\n    num_cols <- length(column_names)\n    out <- c(out, \"|-\", paste0('|colspan=\"', num_cols, '\"|', source_text))\n  }\n\n  out <- c(out, \"|}\")\n  paste(out, collapse = \"\\n\")\n}\n\n# ---- Category helpers (new) ---------------------------------------------------\n\n#' Get all members of a Wikipedia category (pages and/or subcategories)\n#'\n#' @param category Category name (with or without \"Category:\" prefix)\n#' @param type \"page\", \"subcat\", or \"page|subcat\"\n#' @param lang Language code\n#' @return tibble with columns: pageid, ns, title\nget_category_members <- function(category, type = \"page\", lang = \"en\") {\n  if (!grepl(\"^Category:\", category)) {\n    category <- paste0(\"Category:\", category)\n  }\n  api_url <- paste0(\"https://\", lang, \".wikipedia.org/w/api.php\")\n\n  all_members <- list()\n  cmcontinue <- NULL\n\n\n  repeat {\n    params <- list(\n      action = \"query\",\n      list = \"categorymembers\",\n      cmtitle = category,\n      cmtype = type,\n      cmlimit = \"500\",\n      format = \"json\"\n    )\n    if (!is.null(cmcontinue)) params$cmcontinue <- cmcontinue\n\n    resp <- httr::GET(api_url, query = params,\n                      user_agent(\"R-wikipedia-tools/1.0\"))\n    json <- fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"),\n                     simplifyVector = FALSE)\n\n    members <- json$query$categorymembers\n    if (length(members) > 0) {\n      all_members <- c(all_members, members)\n    }\n\n    cmcontinue <- json$`continue`$cmcontinue\n    if (is.null(cmcontinue)) break\n  }\n\n  if (length(all_members) == 0) return(tibble(pageid = integer(),\n                                              ns = integer(),\n                                              title = character()))\n  bind_rows(lapply(all_members, function(m) {\n    tibble(pageid = m$pageid, ns = m$ns, title = m$title)\n  }))\n}\n\n#' Get subcategories of a category\nget_subcategories <- function(category, lang = \"en\") {\n  get_category_members(category, type = \"subcat\", lang = lang)\n}\n\n#' Get article pages in a category (non-recursive)\nget_category_pages <- function(category, lang = \"en\") {\n  get_category_members(category, type = \"page\", lang = lang)\n}\n\n# ---- Batch page info ----------------------------------------------------------\n\n#' Fetch basic page info + Wikidata QID for a vector of titles\n#'\n#' @param titles Character vector of page titles\n#' @param lang Language code\n#' @return tibble with: title, pageid, length, wikidata_qid\nget_page_info_batch <- function(titles, lang = \"en\") {\n  api_url <- paste0(\"https://\", lang, \".wikipedia.org/w/api.php\")\n  results <- list()\n\n  # API accepts up to 50 titles at once\n\n  batches <- split(titles, ceiling(seq_along(titles) / 50))\n\n  for (batch in batches) {\n    params <- list(\n      action = \"query\",\n      prop = \"pageprops|info\",\n      titles = paste(batch, collapse = \"|\"),\n      format = \"json\",\n      ppprop = \"wikibase_item\"\n    )\n\n    resp <- httr::GET(api_url, query = params,\n                      user_agent(\"R-wikipedia-tools/1.0\"))\n    json <- fromJSON(content(resp, \"text\", encoding = \"UTF-8\"),\n                     simplifyVector = FALSE)\n\n    pages <- json$query$pages\n    for (p in pages) {\n      results <- c(results, list(tibble(\n        title = p$title %||% NA_character_,\n        pageid = p$pageid %||% NA_integer_,\n        page_length = p$length %||% NA_integer_,\n        wikidata_qid = p$pageprops$wikibase_item %||% NA_character_\n      )))\n    }\n    Sys.sleep(0.2)\n  }\n\n  bind_rows(results)\n}\n\n# ---- Wikitext parsing helpers --------------------------------------------------\n\n#' Extract the raw wikitext of the main infobox template\n#'\n#' Isolates the complete infobox template (from {{ to matching }}) from wikitext.\n#' Handles nested templates via recursive brace matching.\n#' Returns NULL if no infobox found.\n#'\n#' @param wikitext The raw wikitext string\n#' @return A string containing the full infobox template wikitext, or NULL\nextract_infobox_wikitext <- function(wikitext) {\n  if (is.null(wikitext)) return(NULL)\n\n  # Find the start of an infobox template\n  infobox_start <- regexpr(\"\\\\{\\\\{\\\\s*[Ii]nfobox\", wikitext)\n  if (infobox_start == -1) return(NULL)\n\n  # Walk forward from start, counting braces to find the matching close\n  txt <- substring(wikitext, infobox_start)\n  depth <- 0\n  end_pos <- NA\n  i <- 1\n  while (i <= nchar(txt)) {\n    ch <- substr(txt, i, i)\n    if (ch == \"{\" && i < nchar(txt) && substr(txt, i + 1, i + 1) == \"{\") {\n      depth <- depth + 1\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n    if (ch == \"}\" && i < nchar(txt) && substr(txt, i + 1, i + 1) == \"}\") {\n      depth <- depth - 1\n      if (depth == 0) {\n        end_pos <- i + 1\n        break\n      }\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n    i <- i + 1\n  }\n\n  if (is.na(end_pos)) return(NULL)\n  substr(txt, 1, end_pos)\n}\n\n#' Extract the main infobox from wikitext as a named list\n#'\n#' Parses the raw infobox wikitext (obtained via extract_infobox_wikitext)\n#' into a named list of field-value pairs.\n#' Returns NULL if no infobox found.\nextract_infobox <- function(wikitext) {\n  if (is.null(wikitext)) return(NULL)\n\n  infobox_text <- extract_infobox_wikitext(wikitext)\n  if (is.null(infobox_text)) return(NULL)\n\n  # Parse pipe-delimited parameters\n  # Remove the outer {{ and }} and the template name line\n  inner <- sub(\"^\\\\{\\\\{\\\\s*[Ii]nfobox[^\\\\n|]*\", \"\", infobox_text)\n  inner <- sub(\"\\\\}\\\\}$\", \"\", inner)\n\n  # Split on top-level pipes (not inside nested {{ }})\n  params <- split_on_top_level_pipes(inner)\n\n  result <- list()\n  for (param in params) {\n    param <- str_trim(param)\n    if (param == \"\" || !grepl(\"=\", param)) next\n\n    # Split on first \"=\"\n    eq_pos <- regexpr(\"=\", param)\n    key <- str_trim(substr(param, 1, eq_pos - 1))\n    val <- str_trim(substr(param, eq_pos + 1, nchar(param)))\n\n    if (nchar(key) > 0) {\n      result[[key]] <- val\n    }\n  }\n  result\n}\n\n#' Extract infobox as a named list of cleaned text values\n#'\n#' @param wikitext The raw wikitext string\n#' @return A named character vector of cleaned values, or NULL if no infobox found\nextract_infobox_as_list <- function(wikitext) {\n  infobox_raw <- extract_infobox(wikitext)\n  if (is.null(infobox_raw)) return(NULL)\n\n  # Clean all values and remove those that become empty/NA\n  cleaned <- map_chr(infobox_raw, clean_infobox_value)\n\n  # Keep only non-empty/non-NA elements\n  keep <- !is.na(cleaned) & cleaned != \"\"\n\n  if (!any(keep)) return(NULL)\n\n  # Return as a named character vector\n  as.character(cleaned[keep][names(cleaned)[keep]])\n}\n\n#' Split a string on \"|\" characters that are not inside {{ }}\nsplit_on_top_level_pipes <- function(text) {\n  parts <- character()\n  depth <- 0\n  current <- \"\"\n\n  i <- 1\n  while (i <= nchar(text)) {\n    ch <- substr(text, i, i)\n\n    if (ch == \"{\" && i < nchar(text) && substr(text, i + 1, i + 1) == \"{\") {\n      depth <- depth + 1\n      current <- paste0(current, \"{{\")\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n    if (ch == \"}\" && i < nchar(text) && substr(text, i + 1, i + 1) == \"}\") {\n      depth <- depth - 1\n      current <- paste0(current, \"}}\")\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n    # Also handle [[ ]] nesting\n    if (ch == \"[\" && i < nchar(text) && substr(text, i + 1, i + 1) == \"[\") {\n      depth <- depth + 1\n      current <- paste0(current, \"[[\")\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n    if (ch == \"]\" && i < nchar(text) && substr(text, i + 1, i + 1) == \"]\") {\n      depth <- depth - 1\n      current <- paste0(current, \"]]\")\n      i <- i + 2\n      next\n    }\n\n    if (ch == \"|\" && depth == 0) {\n      parts <- c(parts, current)\n      current <- \"\"\n    } else {\n      current <- paste0(current, ch)\n    }\n    i <- i + 1\n  }\n  if (nchar(current) > 0) parts <- c(parts, current)\n  parts\n}\n\n#' Clean wikitext markup from an infobox value\n#'\n#' Strips wikilinks, templates, HTML tags, and refs, leaving plain text.\nclean_infobox_value <- function(val) {\n  if (is.null(val) || is.na(val)) return(NA_character_)\n  # Remove <ref>...</ref> and <ref />\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"(?s)<ref[^>]*>.*?</ref>\", \"\")\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"<ref[^/]*/\\\\s*>\", \"\")\n  # Remove HTML tags\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"<[^>]+>\", \"\")\n  # Resolve wikilinks: [[Target|Display]] -> Display, [[Link]] -> Link\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\[\\\\[(?:[^|\\\\]]*\\\\|)?([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\", \"\\\\1\")\n  # Resolve common simple templates before stripping all templates:\n  # {{flag|X}} -> X, {{flagicon|X}} -> X, {{convert|N|unit}} -> N unit\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\{\\\\{(?:flag|flagicon|flagcountry)\\\\|([^{}|]+)(?:\\\\|[^{}]*)??\\\\}\\\\}\", \"\\\\1\")\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\{\\\\{convert\\\\|([^{}|]+)\\\\|([^{}|]+)(?:\\\\|[^{}]*)?\\\\}\\\\}\", \"\\\\1 \\\\2\")\n  # {{nowrap|X}} -> X\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\{\\\\{nowrap\\\\|([^{}]+)\\\\}\\\\}\", \"\\\\1\")\n  # Remove remaining templates (nested)\n  val <- gsub(\"\\\\{\\\\{(?:[^{}]|(?R))*\\\\}\\\\}\", \"\", val, perl = TRUE)\n  # Remove external links [url text] -> text\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\[https?://\\\\S+\\\\s+([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\", \"\\\\1\")\n  val <- str_replace_all(val, \"\\\\[https?://\\\\S+\\\\]\", \"\")\n  # Clean whitespace\n  val <- str_trim(str_squish(val))\n  if (nchar(val) == 0) NA_character_ else val\n}\n\n#' Count citation templates in wikitext\ncount_citations <- function(wikitext) {\n  if (is.null(wikitext)) return(0L)\n  # Count {{cite ...}} and {{Citation}} templates\n  cite_pattern <- \"\\\\{\\\\{\\\\s*[Cc]it(e|ation)\\\\s\"\n  length(str_extract_all(wikitext, cite_pattern)[[1]])\n}\n\n#' Count total <ref> tags in wikitext (includes non-templated refs)\ncount_refs <- function(wikitext) {\n  if (is.null(wikitext)) return(0L)\n  # Count opening <ref> tags (both <ref> and <ref name=...>)\n  ref_pattern <- \"<ref[\\\\s>]\"\n  # Also count self-closing <ref name=\"...\" />\n  self_closing <- \"<ref\\\\s[^>]*/>\"\n  length(str_extract_all(wikitext, ref_pattern)[[1]]) +\n    length(str_extract_all(wikitext, self_closing)[[1]])\n}\n\n#' Extract census years referenced in wikitext\n#'\n#' Looks for patterns like \"census of YYYY\", \"YYYY census\", \"CPV YYYY\",\n#' and \"Censo YYYY\" (common in Bolivian municipality articles).\nextract_census_years <- function(wikitext) {\n  if (is.null(wikitext)) return(character(0))\n  patterns <- c(\n    \"(?i)\\\\b(\\\\d{4})\\\\s+census\\\\b\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\b(\\\\d{4})\\\\s+\\\\w+\\\\s+census\\\\b\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\bcensus\\\\s+(?:of\\\\s+)?(\\\\d{4})\\\\b\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\bcenso\\\\s+(?:de\\\\s+)?(\\\\d{4})\\\\b\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\bCPV\\\\s+(\\\\d{4})\\\\b\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\bcensus_year\\\\s*=\\\\s*(\\\\d{4})\",\n    \"(?i)\\\\bpopulation_as_of\\\\s*=\\\\s*(\\\\d{4})\"\n  )\n  years <- character(0)\n  for (pat in patterns) {\n    matches <- str_match_all(wikitext, pat)[[1]]\n    if (nrow(matches) > 0) {\n      # The year is in the first capture group\n      extracted <- matches[, 2]\n      years <- c(years, extracted)\n    }\n  }\n  sort(unique(years[!is.na(years) & nchar(years) == 4]))\n}\n\n# ---- Caching helpers -----------------------------------------------------------\n\n#' Cache wikitext to a local file; load from cache if available\n#'\n#' @param title Page title\n#' @param cache_dir Directory path for cached files\n#' @param lang Language code\n#' @return Wikitext string (NULL if page doesn't exist)\ncache_wikitext <- function(title, cache_dir, lang = \"en\") {\n  # Sanitise filename: replace / and other problematic chars\n  safe_name <- gsub(\"[/:*?\\\"<>|]\", \"_\", title)\n  cache_path <- file.path(cache_dir, paste0(safe_name, \".txt\"))\n\n  if (file.exists(cache_path)) {\n    return(paste(readLines(cache_path, warn = FALSE), collapse = \"\\n\"))\n  }\n\n  wikitext <- get_wikitext_by_name(title, lang = lang)\n  if (!is.null(wikitext)) {\n    writeLines(wikitext, cache_path)\n  }\n  wikitext\n}\n"},"elapsedMs":79}</script></tool-result></div>

font-size: 13px;\")\n}\n\nfacility_block <- function(cid, facility_name, facility_city, facility_state,\n                           wiki_slug = NA_character_) {\n  ib <- generate_infobox(\n    cid,\n    panel_facilities = cf,\n    presence = fp,\n    geocoded = geo,\n    panel = panel,\n    crosswalk = xwalk,\n    wiki_matches = fw\n  )\n\n  bar <- generate_adp_bar_chart(cid, panel, fp)\n\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(facility_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" — \", facility_city, \", \", facility_state),\n          style = \"color: #586069;\"\n        ),\n        wiki_link(wiki_slug)\n      ),\n      wikitext_block(paste0(\"ib_\", cid), \"Copy Infobox\", ib),\n      if (nzchar(bar)) wikitext_block(paste0(\"bar_\", cid), \"Copy Bar Chart\", bar)\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e1e4e8; margin: 6px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\nrender_facility_blocks <- function(df) {\n  blocks <- purrr::pmap(\n    df |> select(canonical_id, facility_name, facility_city, facility_state,\n                 wiki_slug),\n    \\(canonical_id, facility_name, facility_city, facility_state, wiki_slug)\n      facility_block(canonical_id, facility_name, facility_city, facility_state,\n                     wiki_slug)\n  )\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: classify-facilities\n#| include: false\n\nhas_article <- fw |>\n  filter(!is.na(wiki_slug), wiki_slug != \"\") |>\n  arrange(facility_name)\n\nhas_match_only <- fw |>\n  filter((is.na(wiki_slug) | wiki_slug == \"\"),\n         !is.na(wiki_match), wiki_match != \"\") |>\n  arrange(facility_name)\n\nredlinks <- fw |>\n  filter(is.na(wiki_slug) | wiki_slug == \"\",\n         is.na(wiki_match) | wiki_match == \"\") |>\n  arrange(facility_name)\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: copy-assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n## Existing Wikipedia articles\n\n**`r nrow(has_article)`** facilities with a confirmed Wikipedia article. Use these to add or update infoboxes and population charts on existing pages.\n\n```{r}\n#| label: existing-articles\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_facility_blocks(has_article)\n```\n\n## Unmatched facilities with a fuzzy Wikipedia match\n\n**`r nrow(has_match_only)`** facilities matched to a Wikipedia title by name similarity but not confirmed as the correct article. Check the linked page before using.\n\n```{r}\n#| label: fuzzy-matches\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\n\n# Show the fuzzy wiki_match as the link for review\nrender_facility_blocks(\n  has_match_only |> mutate(wiki_slug = wiki_match)\n)\n```\n\n## Red-link facilities\n\n**`r nrow(redlinks)`** facilities with no Wikipedia article or match. Each section contains wikitext ready to paste into a new article.\n\n```{r}\n#| label: redlinks\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_facility_blocks(redlinks)\n```\n"},"elapsedMs":1961}</script></tool-result></div>

The Hidden Storage\n    # We use a textarea because it preserves line breaks and whitespace perfectly\n    htmltools::tags$textarea(\n      id = table_id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      raw_markdown\n    )\n  )\n}\n"},"elapsedMs":3380}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01UvHHoQmjgSLqsE3cAag9qe"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"# ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n\n#' Form...[truncated, total 137 chars]","new_string":"# ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n\n#' Buil...[truncated, total 2730 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -185,6 +185,80 @@\n   out\n }\n \n+# ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n+\n+#' Build a MediaWiki council table for a single Bolivian municipality\n+#'\n+#' Produces a wikitable with rowspan=\"2\" bridging the Titular and Suplente rows\n+#' for each council seat. IOC (indigenous) seats display the pueblo name in the\n+#' party column. Names are converted to title case.\n+#'\n+#' @param id_muni_code  Six-digit id_muni string (e.g. \"010101\")\n+#' @param data          Data frame in the shape of muni_concejo_comb; must\n+#'                      contain columns: id_muni, silla, tipo, nombre, sigla,\n+#'                      esp_ioc, pueblo, pendiente\n+#' @param caption       Optional wikitable caption string\n+#' @return A character string of MediaWiki wikitext\n+build_council_wikitable <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb,\n+                                    caption = NULL) {\n+  df <- data |>\n+    filter(id_muni == id_muni_code) |>\n+    arrange(silla, if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", 1L, 2L))\n+\n+  if (nrow(df) == 0) return(\"\")\n+\n+  # Name cell: title case, or italic \"Por definir\" when no person is assigned\n+  nombre_cell <- function(row) {\n+    if (isTRUE(row$pendiente) || is.na(row$nombre) || row$nombre == \"\") {\n+      \"''Por definir''\"\n+    } else {\n+      str_to_title(row$nombre)\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  # Party/organisation cell: sigla for regular seats, pueblo name for IOC seats\n+  partido_cell <- function(row) {\n+    if (isTRUE(row$esp_ioc) && !is.na(row$pueblo) && row$pueblo != \"\") {\n+      str_to_title(row$pueblo)\n+    } else if (!is.na(row$sigla) && row$sigla != \"\") {\n+      row$sigla\n+    } else {\n+      \"\"\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  lines <- '{| class=\"wikitable\"'\n+  if (!is.null(caption)) lines <- c(lines, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n+  lines <- c(lines, \"! N.\\u00b0 !! Tipo !! Nombre !! Partido / Organizaci\\u00f3n Pol\\u00edtica\")\n+\n+  for (s in unique(df$silla)) {\n+    seat <- df[df$silla == s, ]\n+    n    <- nrow(seat)\n+\n+    for (i in seq_len(n)) {\n+      row  <- seat[i, ]\n+      tipo <- if (row$tipo == \"TITULAR\") \"Titular\" else \"Suplente\"\n+      nom  <- nombre_cell(row)\n+      part <- partido_cell(row)\n+\n+      lines <- c(lines, \"|-\")\n+\n+      if (n == 2 && i == 1) {\n+        lines <- c(lines, sprintf('| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n+                                   s, tipo, nom, part))\n+      } else if (n == 2 && i == 2) {\n+        lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\", tipo, nom, part))\n+      } else {\n+        # Unpaired seat — no rowspan\n+        lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %d || %s || %s || %s\", s, tipo, nom, part))\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  lines <- c(lines, \"|}\")\n+  paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n+}\n+\n # ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n \n #' Format a data frame as a MediaWiki wikitable string\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01KHWPuZzFqLyfLXwazi5sUt"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","content":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para co...[truncated, total 2652 chars]","overwrite":true},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para copiar a Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Renders a button + visible wikitext block + hidden textarea for clipboard copy\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$div(\n      style = \"margin: 8px 0;\",\n      tags$button(\n        class = \"copy-btn\",\n        onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n        label\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 12px; white-space: pre;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders a collapsible <details> block for one municipality\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, council_wikitext) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      wikitext_block(\n        id    = paste0(\"concejo_\", id_muni),\n        label = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\",\n        content = council_wikitext\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e1e4e8; margin: 4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  blocks <- pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    wt <- build_council_wikitable(id_muni, data = concejo)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, wt)\n  })\n\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n","language":"markdown"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Works correctly. The rowspan logic fires for every paired seat, names are in title case, and siglas stay uppercase.

One minor thing to note: `str_to_title()` capitalises every word including particles like "De" in "Ponce **De** León" — that's standard Wikipedia practice for proper names so it's fine.

**Summary of what was built:**

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R`** — new `build_council_wikitable(id_muni_code, data, caption)`:
- Filters `muni_concejo_comb` for the given municipality
- Guarantees TITULAR before SUPLENTE ordering for each seat
- Emits `rowspan="2"` on the seat number cell when both members are present; falls back to a single row if only one is present
- IOC seats use the `pueblo` name in the party column; pending IOC members get `''Por definir''` in italics
- Names converted to title case; siglas preserved as-is

**`bolivia-muni-generator.qmd`** — rebuilt from scratch:
- Loads `muni_id_lookup_table` and `muni_concejo_comb` from local RDS files (no targets dependency)
- Iterates over all municipalities sorted by `id_muni`, skipping any without council data
- One collapsible `<details>` block per municipality showing the OEP name (`muni_anexo`), department, and `id_muni` code
- Copy button wired to the existing `copyTableToClipboard` JavaScript from `src/gh-copy-assets.R`

p2 <- partido_cell(seat[2, ])\n+      p1 != \"\" && identical(p1, p2)\n+    }\n+\n     for (i in seq_len(n)) {\n       row  <- seat[i, ]\n       tipo <- if (row$tipo == \"TITULAR\") \"Titular\" else \"Suplente\"\n       nom  <- nombre_cell(row)\n+      # Suplente names rendered in wikitext italics\n+      if (row$tipo == \"SUPLENTE\") nom <- paste0(\"''\", nom, \"''\")\n       part <- partido_cell(row)\n \n       lines <- c(lines, \"|-\")\n \n       if (n == 2 && i == 1) {\n-        lines <- c(lines, sprintf('| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n-                                   s, tipo, nom, part))\n+        if (same_party) {\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n+            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || rowspan=\"2\" | %s',\n+            s, tipo, nom, part))\n+        } else {\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n+            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n+            s, tipo, nom, part))\n+        }\n       } else if (n == 2 && i == 2) {\n-        lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\", tipo, nom, part))\n+        if (same_party) {\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s\", tipo, nom))\n+        } else {\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\", tipo, nom, part))\n+        }\n       } else {\n         # Unpaired seat — no rowspan\n         lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %d || %s || %s || %s\", s, tipo, nom, part))\n@@ -259,6 +280,40 @@\n   paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n }\n \n+#' Build an English HTML kable preview of a municipal council\n+#'\n+#' Returns an HTML fragment (htmltools::HTML) suitable for embedding in a\n+#' Quarto HTML document. Both functions work correctly. Here's a summary of all changes:

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R` — `build_council_wikitable()`:**
- **Party rowspan**: precomputes `same_party` for each seat before the inner loop. When both members share a party, the titular row gets `rowspan="2" | PARTY` and the suplente row emits only two cells instead of three. (As it turns out, every seat in the data has matching titular/suplente parties — proportional representation — so this fires universally.)
- **Suplente italics**: `''name''` is applied to all suplente names after `nombre_cell()` resolves them. "Por definir" is a plain string now (no pre-wrapped quotes), so the italic pass applies uniformly.

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R` — new `build_council_kable()`:**
- English headers: Seat, Type, Name, Party/Political Organization
- Suplente names wrapped in `<em>` tags; TBD for missing members
- Returns `htmltools::HTML` suitable for embedding directly in htmltools tagLists

**`bolivia-muni-generator.qmd` — `muni_block()`:**
- Now calls `build_council_kable()` and renders the English HTML table above the wikitext copy block inside each `<details>` section.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Cuj8Z31YhQ7JdfBet2QxK1"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"read","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","offset":188,"limit":120},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R:188-307","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R:188"}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01Cuj8Z31YhQ7JdfBet2QxK1"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 23.6KB, 707 lines, showing lines 188-307 (truncated)]\n   188→# ---- Bolivia municip...[truncated, total 5300 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 23.6KB, 707 lines, showing lines 188-307 (truncated)]\n# ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n\n#' Build a MediaWiki council table for a single Bolivian municipality\n#'\n#' Produces a Spanish-language wikitable. The seat number cell spans both the\n#' Titular and Suplente rows (rowspan=\"2\"). When both members of a seat share the\n#' same party, the party cell also spans both rows. Suplente names are wrapped in\n#' wikitext italics (''...'').  IOC (indigenous) seats show the pueblo name in the\n#' party column. Names are converted to title case.\n#'\n#' @param id_muni_code  Six-digit id_muni string (e.g. \"010101\")\n#' @param data          Data frame in the shape of muni_concejo_comb; must\n#'                      contain columns: id_muni, silla, tipo, nombre, sigla,\n#'                      esp_ioc, pueblo, pendiente\n#' @param caption       Optional wikitable caption string\n#' @return A character string of MediaWiki wikitext\nbuild_council_wikitable <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb,\n                                    caption = NULL) {\n  df <- data |>\n    filter(id_muni == id_muni_code) |>\n    arrange(silla, if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", 1L, 2L))\n\n  if (nrow(df) == 0) return(\"\")\n\n  # Plain name, title case; \"Por definir\" for missing/pending members\n  nombre_cell <- function(row) {\n    if (isTRUE(row$pendiente) || is.na(row$nombre) || row$nombre == \"\") {\n      \"Por definir\"\n    } else {\n      str_to_title(row$nombre)\n    }\n  }\n\n  # Party/organisation: sigla for regular seats, pueblo name for IOC seats\n  partido_cell <- function(row) {\n    if (isTRUE(row$esp_ioc) && !is.na(row$pueblo) && row$pueblo != \"\") {\n      str_to_title(row$pueblo)\n    } else if (!is.na(row$sigla) && row$sigla != \"\") {\n      row$sigla\n    } else {\n      \"\"\n    }\n  }\n\n  lines <- '{| class=\"wikitable\"'\n  if (!is.null(caption)) lines <- c(lines, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n  lines <- c(lines, \"! N.\\u00b0 !! Tipo !! Nombre !! Partido / Organizaci\\u00f3n Pol\\u00edtica\")\n\n  for (s in unique(df$silla)) {\n    seat <- df[df$silla == s, ]\n    n    <- nrow(seat)\n\n    # For paired seats, check whether both members share the same party\n    same_party <- n == 2 && {\n      p1 <- partido_cell(seat[1, ]); p2 <- partido_cell(seat[2, ])\n      p1 != \"\" && identical(p1, p2)\n    }\n\n    for (i in seq_len(n)) {\n      row  <- seat[i, ]\n      tipo <- if (row$tipo == \"TITULAR\") \"Titular\" else \"Suplente\"\n      nom  <- nombre_cell(row)\n      # Suplente names rendered in wikitext italics\n      if (row$tipo == \"SUPLENTE\") nom <- paste0(\"''\", nom, \"''\")\n      part <- partido_cell(row)\n\n      lines <- c(lines, \"|-\")\n\n      if (n == 2 && i == 1) {\n        if (same_party) {\n          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || rowspan=\"2\" | %s',\n            s, tipo, nom, part))\n        } else {\n          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n            s, tipo, nom, part))\n        }\n      } else if (n == 2 && i == 2) {\n        if (same_party) {\n          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s\", tipo, nom))\n        } else {\n          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\", tipo, nom, part))\n        }\n      } else {\n        # Unpaired seat — no rowspan\n        lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %d || %s || %s || %s\", s, tipo, nom, part))\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  lines <- c(lines, \"|}\")\n  paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n}\n\n#' Build an English HTML kable preview of a municipal council\n#'\n#' Returns an HTML fragment (htmltools::HTML) suitable for embedding in a\n#' Quarto HTML document.  Suplente names are displayed in italics.\n#'\n#' @param id_muni_code  Six-digit id_muni string\n#' @param data          Data frame in the shape of muni_concejo_comb\n#' @return An htmltools::HTML object\nbuild_council_kable <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb) {\n  df <- data |>\n    filter(id_muni == id_muni_code) |>\n    arrange(silla, if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", 1L, 2L)) |>\n    mutate(\n      Name = case_when(\n        isTRUE(pendiente) | is.na(nombre) | nombre == \"\" ~ \"<em>TBD</em>\",\n        tipo == \"SUPLENTE\" ~ paste0(\"<em>\", str_to_title(nombre), \"</em>\"),\n        TRUE ~ str_to_title(nombre)\n      ),\n      Party = case_when(\n        isTRUE(esp_ioc) & !is.na(pueblo) & pueblo != \"\" ~ str_to_title(pueblo),\n        !is.na(sigla) & sigla != \"\" ~ sigla,\n        TRUE ~ \"\"\n      ),\n      Type = if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", \"Titular\", \"Suplente\")\n    ) |>"},"elapsedMs":80}</script></tool-result></div>
