<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01HKabjjXkwP33nkKosrNUqD"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 23.6KB, 707 lines, showing lines 307-331 (truncated)]\n   307→    ) |>\n   308→    se...[truncated, total 1211 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 23.6KB, 707 lines, showing lines 307-331 (truncated)]\n    ) |>\n    select(Seat = silla, Type, Name,\n           `Party / Political Organization` = Party)\n\n  htmltools::HTML(\n    knitr::kable(df, format = \"html\", escape = FALSE,\n                 table.attr = 'class=\"table table-sm table-striped\" style=\"font-size:13px;width:auto;\"')\n  )\n}\n\n# ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n\n#' Format a data frame as a MediaWiki wikitable string\nget_wikitable <- function(df, caption = NULL, class = \"wikitable sortable\",\n                          column_names = NULL, source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n  out <- c()\n  out <- c(out, paste0('{| class=\"', class, '\"'))\n  if (!is.null(caption)) out <- c(out, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n\n  if (is.null(column_names)) column_names <- names(df)\n  out <- c(out, paste0(\"! \", paste(column_names, collapse = \" !! \")))\n\n  formatted_rows <- apply(df, 1, function(row) {\n    row_clean <- gsub(\"\\\\|\", \"|\", as.character(row))\n    row_clean[is.na(row_clean)] <- \"\""},"elapsedMs":58}</script></tool-result></div>

<span class="thinking-topic">Refactoring into language-parameterized functions</span>



Now I have a clear picture of what to replace. I'll:
1. Replace both existing functions with a single internal function that takes `lang`
2. Add `_es` and `_en` wrappers
3. Keep the old names as aliases to `_es` and `_en` respectively for backward compatibility
4. Update the QMD for side-by-side layout

Let me write the replacement block now.

For the side-by-side layout in the QMD, I'll update `muni_block` to use flexbox. The wikitext `<pre>` should have `overflow-x: scroll` or `overflow-x: auto` so it doesn't overflow its flex column.
<br>
Now I'm thinking about the font sizing—I want the wikitext proportionally smaller than the kable. I'll set the kable to 12px and the wikitext `<pre>` to 11px, then use a flex container with proper gap and alignment to display them side-by-side.

Writing the layout structure...
</thinking><span data-thinking-duration-ms="10498"></span></div>

Now I have the full picture. I'll replace both functions with language-parameterized internals plus `_es`/`_en` wrappers, then update the QMD layout:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01VwDoGUpzvYaP6cY5c52Az6"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"# ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n\n#' Buil...[truncated, total 4674 chars]","new_string":"# ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n\n# Langu...[truncated, total 5158 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -187,38 +187,46 @@\n \n # ---- Bolivia municipal council wikitable --------------------------------------\n \n-#' Build a MediaWiki council table for a single Bolivian municipality\n-#'\n-#' Produces a Spanish-language wikitable. The seat number cell spans both the\n-#' Titular and Suplente rows (rowspan=\"2\"). When both members of a seat share the\n-#' same party, the party cell also spans both rows. Suplente names are wrapped in\n-#' wikitext italics (''...'').  IOC (indigenous) seats show the pueblo name in the\n-#' party column. Names are converted to title case.\n-#'\n-#' @param id_muni_code  Six-digit id_muni string (e.g. \"010101\")\n-#' @param data          Data frame in the shape of muni_concejo_comb; must\n-#'                      contain columns: id_muni, silla, tipo, nombre, sigla,\n-#'                      esp_ioc, pueblo, pendiente\n-#' @param caption       Optional wikitable caption string\n-#' @return A character string of MediaWiki wikitext\n-build_council_wikitable <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb,\n-                                    caption = NULL) {\n+# Language label sets used by both the wikitable and kable builders.\n+.council_labels <- list(\n+  es = list(\n+    seat    = \"N.\\u00b0\",\n+    type    = \"Tipo\",\n+    name    = \"Nombre\",\n+    party   = \"Partido / Organizaci\\u00f3n Pol\\u00edtica\",\n+    member  = \"Titular\",\n+    alt     = \"Suplente\",\n+    pending = \"Por definir\"\n+  ),\n+  en = list(\n+    seat    = \"Seat\",\n+    type    = \"Type\",\n+    name    = \"Name\",\n+    party   = \"Party / Political Organization\",\n+    member  = \"Member\",\n+    alt     = \"Alternate\",\n+    pending = \"TBD\"\n+  )\n+)\n+\n+# Internal: build council wikitable for a given language\n+.build_council_wikitable <- function(id_muni_code, data, caption, lang) {\n+  lb <- .council_labels[[lang]]\n+\n   df <- data |>\n     filter(id_muni == id_muni_code) |>\n     arrange(silla, if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", 1L, 2L))\n \n   if (nrow(df) == 0) return(\"\")\n \n-  # Plain name, title case; \"Por definir\" for missing/pending members\n   nombre_cell <- function(row) {\n     if (isTRUE(row$pendiente) || is.na(row$nombre) || row$nombre == \"\") {\n-      \"Por definir\"\n+      lb$pending\n     } else {\n       str_to_title(row$nombre)\n     }\n   }\n \n-  # Party/organisation: sigla for regular seats, pueblo name for IOC seats\n   partido_cell <- function(row) {\n     if (isTRUE(row$esp_ioc) && !is.na(row$pueblo) && row$pueblo != \"\") {\n       str_to_title(row$pueblo)\n@@ -231,13 +239,13 @@\n \n   lines <- '{| class=\"wikitable\"'\n   if (!is.null(caption)) lines <- c(lines, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n-  lines <- c(lines, \"! N.\\u00b0 !! Tipo !! Nombre !! Partido / Organizaci\\u00f3n Pol\\u00edtica\")\n+  lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"! %s !! %s !! %s !! %s\",\n+                             lb$seat, lb$type, lb$name, lb$party))\n \n   for (s in unique(df$silla)) {\n     seat <- df[df$silla == s, ]\n     n    <- nrow(seat)\n \n-    # For paired seats, check whether both members share the same party\n     same_party <- n == 2 && {\n       p1 <- partido_cell(seat[1, ]); p2 <- partido_cell(seat[2, ])\n       p1 != \"\" && identical(p1, p2)\n@@ -245,9 +253,8 @@\n \n     for (i in seq_len(n)) {\n       row  <- seat[i, ]\n-      tipo <- if (row$tipo == \"TITULAR\") \"Titular\" else \"Suplente\"\n+      tipo <- if (row$tipo == \"TITULAR\") lb$member else lb$alt\n       nom  <- nombre_cell(row)\n-      # Suplente names rendered in wikitext italics\n       if (row$tipo == \"SUPLENTE\") nom <- paste0(\"''\", nom, \"''\")\n       part <- partido_cell(row)\n \n@@ -255,13 +262,11 @@\n \n       if (n == 2 && i == 1) {\n         if (same_party) {\n-          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n-            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || rowspan=\"2\" | %s',\n-            s, tipo, nom, part))\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf('| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || rowspan=\"2\" | %s',\n+                                     s, tipo, nom, part))\n         } else {\n-          lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\n-            '| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n-            s, tipo, nom, part))\n+          lines <- c(lines, sprintf('| rowspan=\"2\" | %d || %s || %s || %s',\n+                                     s, tipo, nom, part))\n         }\n       } else if (n == 2 && i == 2) {\n         if (same_party) {\n@@ -270,7 +275,6 @@\n           lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\", tipo, nom, part))\n         }\n       } else {\n-        # Unpaired seat — no rowspan\n         lines <- c(lines, sprintf(\"| %d || %s || %s || %s\", s, tipo, nom, part))\n       }\n     }\n@@ -280,40 +284,71 @@\n   paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n }\n \n-#' Build an English HTML kable preview of a municipal council\n-#'\n-#' Returns an HTML fragment (htmltools::HTML) suitable for embedding in a\n-#' Quarto HTML document.  Suplente names are displayed in italics.\n-#'\n-#' @param id_muni_code  Six-digit id_muni string\n-#' @param data          Data frame in the shape of muni_concejo_comb\n-#' @return An htmltools::HTML object\n-build_council_kable <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb) {\n+# Internal: build council kable for a given language\n+.build_council_kable <- function(id_muni_code, data, lang) {\n+  lb <- .council_labels[[lang]]\n+\n   df <- data |>\n     filter(id_muni == id_muni_code) |>\n     arrange(silla, if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", 1L, 2L)) |>\n     mutate(\n-      Name = case_when(\n-        isTRUE(pendiente) | is.na(nombre) | nombre == \"\" ~ \"<em>TBD</em>\",\n+      .name = case_when(\n+        isTRUE(pendiente) | is.na(nombre) | nombre == \"\" ~\n+          paste0(\"<em>\", lb$pending, \"</em>\"),\n         tipo == \"SUPLENTE\" ~ paste0(\"<em>\", str_to_title(nombre), \"</em>\"),\n         TRUE ~ str_to_title(nombre)\n       ),\n-      Party = case_when(\n+      .party = case_when(\n         isTRUE(esp_ioc) & !is.na(pueblo) & pueblo != \"\" ~ str_to_title(pueblo),\n         !is.na(sigla) & sigla != \"\" ~ sigla,\n         TRUE ~ \"\"\n       ),\n-      Type = if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", \"Titular\", \"Suplente\")\n-    ) |>\n-    select(Seat = silla, Type, Name,\n-           `Party / Political Organization` = Party)\n+      .type = if_else(tipo == \"TITULAR\", lb$member, lb$alt)\n+    )\n \n+  # Build column-name-safe data frame for kable\n+  out <- data.frame(\n+    seat  = df$silla,\n+    type  = df$.type,\n+    name  = df$.name,\n+    party = df$.party,\n+    stringsAsFactors = FALSE,\n+    check.names = FALSE\n+  )\n+  names(out) <- c(lb$seat, lb$type, lb$name, lb$party)\n+\n   htmltools::HTML(\n-    knitr::kable(df, format = \"html\", escape = FALSE,\n-                 table.attr = 'class=\"table table-sm table-striped\" style=\"font-size:13px;width:auto;\"')\n+    knitr::kable(out, format = \"html\", escape = FALSE,\n+                 table.attr = 'class=\"table table-sm table-striped\" style=\"font-size:11px;width:100%;\"')\n   )\n }\n \n+#' @describeIn build_council_wikitable_es Spanish MediaWiki council table\n+build_council_wikitable_es <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb,\n+                                       caption = NULL) {\n+  .build_council_wikitable(id_muni_code, data, caption, lang = \"es\")\n+}\n+\n+#' @describeIn build_council_wikitable_en English MediaWiki council table\n+build_council_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb,\n+                                       caption = NULL) {\n+  .build_council_wikitable(id_muni_code, data, caption, lang = \"en\")\n+}\n+\n+#' @describeIn build_council_kable_es Spanish HTML kable preview\n+build_council_kable_es <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb) {\n+  .build_council_kable(id_muni_code, data, lang = \"es\")\n+}\n+\n+#' @describeIn build_council_kable_en English HTML kable preview\n+build_council_kable_en <- function(id_muni_code, data = muni_concejo_comb) {\n+  .build_council_kable(id_muni_code, data, lang = \"en\")\n+}\n+\n+# Backward-compatible aliases\n+build_council_wikitable <- build_council_wikitable_es\n+build_council_kable     <- build_council_kable_en\n+\n # ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n \n #' Format a data frame as a MediaWiki wikitable string\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01QoKCmxAG2vEcaStNin6gAR"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 2.8KB, 115 lines, showing lines 1-115]\n     1→---\n     2→title: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tab...[truncated, total 3703 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 2.8KB, 115 lines, showing lines 1-115]\n---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para copiar a Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Renders a button + visible wikitext block + hidden textarea for clipboard copy\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$div(\n      style = \"margin: 8px 0;\",\n      tags$button(\n        class = \"copy-btn\",\n        onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n        label\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 12px; white-space: pre;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders a collapsible <details> block for one municipality.\n# Shows an English kable preview, then a Spanish wikitext copy block.\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, council_wikitext, concejo) {\n  kbl <- build_council_kable(id_muni, data = concejo)\n\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      tags$div(style = \"padding: 8px 0 4px;\", kbl),\n      wikitext_block(\n        id      = paste0(\"concejo_\", id_muni),\n        label   = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\",\n        content = council_wikitext\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e1e4e8; margin: 4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  blocks <- pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    wt <- build_council_wikitable(id_muni, data = concejo)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, wt, concejo)\n  })\n\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n"},"elapsedMs":49}</script></tool-result></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01GszDkuAjo8n64x34Ss4ZbW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","content":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para co...[truncated, total 3086 chars]","overwrite":true},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"---\ntitle: \"Bolivia 2026 — Tablas del Concejo Municipal\"\nsubtitle: \"Wikitext listo para copiar a Wikipedia\"\nformat:\n  html:\n    code-fold: true\n    toc: false\n    self-contained: true\n---\n\n```{r}\n#| label: setup\n#| include: false\n\nlibrary(dplyr)\nlibrary(purrr)\nlibrary(htmltools)\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nsource(\"src/gh-copy-assets.R\")\n\nmuni_lookup  <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"muni_id_lookup_table.rds\"))\nconcejo_data <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"oep2026\", \"muni_concejo_comb.rds\"))\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: helpers\n#| include: false\n\n# Renders a wikitext copy block (button + visible pre + hidden textarea)\nwikitext_block <- function(id, label, content) {\n  tagList(\n    tags$button(\n      class = \"copy-btn\",\n      style = \"margin-bottom: 6px;\",\n      onclick = sprintf(\"copyTableToClipboard('%s', this)\", id),\n      label\n    ),\n    tags$pre(\n      tags$code(content),\n      style = paste(\n        \"background-color: #f6f8fa; padding: 10px; border-radius: 6px;\",\n        \"overflow-x: auto; font-size: 11px; white-space: pre; margin: 0;\"\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$textarea(\n      id = id,\n      style = \"position: absolute; left: -9999px; height: 0; width: 0; overflow: hidden;\",\n      readonly = TRUE,\n      content\n    )\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders a collapsible <details> block for one municipality.\n# Left panel: English kable preview. Right panel: Spanish wikitext + copy button.\nmuni_block <- function(id_muni, muni_name, department, council_wikitext, concejo) {\n  kbl <- build_council_kable_en(id_muni, data = concejo)\n\n  tagList(\n    tags$details(\n      tags$summary(\n        tags$strong(muni_name),\n        tags$span(\n          paste0(\" \\u2014 \", department),\n          style = \"color: #586069; font-weight: normal;\"\n        ),\n        tags$code(\n          paste0(\" [\", id_muni, \"]\"),\n          style = \"font-size: 11px; color: #999; margin-left: 6px;\"\n        )\n      ),\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start; margin-top:8px; font-size:12px;\",\n        tags$div(style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0; overflow-x:auto;\", kbl),\n        tags$div(\n          style = \"flex:1 1 0; min-width:0;\",\n          wikitext_block(\n            id      = paste0(\"concejo_\", id_muni),\n            label   = \"Copiar tabla del concejo\",\n            content = council_wikitext\n          )\n        )\n      )\n    ),\n    tags$hr(style = \"border:none; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8; margin:4px 0;\")\n  )\n}\n\n# Renders all municipality blocks sorted by id_muni\nrender_muni_blocks <- function(lookup, concejo) {\n  ids <- lookup |>\n    filter(id_muni %in% unique(concejo$id_muni)) |>\n    arrange(id_muni) |>\n    select(id_muni, muni_anexo, department)\n\n  blocks <- pmap(ids, function(id_muni, muni_anexo, department) {\n    wt <- build_council_wikitable_es(id_muni, data = concejo)\n    muni_block(id_muni, muni_anexo, department, wt, concejo)\n  })\n\n  tagList(blocks)\n}\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: assets\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\ninclude_table_assets()\n```\n\n```{r}\n#| label: council-tables\n#| echo: false\n#| results: asis\nrender_muni_blocks(muni_lookup, concejo_data)\n```\n","language":"markdown"}}</script></tool-call></div>

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R`:**
- `.council_labels` — a single named list holding all language-specific strings (`seat`, `type`, `name`, `party`, `member`, `alt`, `pending`) for both `"es"` and `"en"`
- `.build_council_wikitable()` / `.build_council_kable()` — internal implementations parameterized by `lang`; no duplication of logic
- Four public functions: `build_council_wikitable_es`, `build_council_wikitable_en`, `build_council_kable_es`, `build_council_kable_en`
- Old names (`build_council_wikitable`, `build_council_kable`) kept as aliases pointing to `_es` and `_en` respectively

**`bolivia-muni-generator.qmd`:**
- `muni_block()` now calls `build_council_kable_en` and `build_council_wikitable_es` explicitly
- Content inside each `<details>` uses `display:flex; gap:14px` to place the English kable and Spanish wikitext side by side at equal width

- Font sizes reduced to 12px (container) / 11px (table and pre)

Both source rows render correctly. Here's what changed across the three files:

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R`:**
- Added `source_label` (`"Fuente"` / `"Source"`) to `.council_labels`
- `.build_council_wikitable` now accepts `source` and `source_refs`; when `source` is non-null it appends `|-` + `|colspan="4"|'''Fuente/Source:''' ...` before `|}`, exactly mirroring `get_wikitable`
- Both `build_council_wikitable_es` and `build_council_wikitable_en` pass the new parameters through

**Both QMD files (source-data chunk identical):**
- New `source-data` chunk loads `muni_roster` page numbers from `oep2026_clean.rds` into `roster_pages`, then removes the full object
- `muni_block` looks up `page` for the current `id_muni` and builds the citation string before calling the wikitable function — `''Elecciones Generales 2025''` in ES, `''General Elections 2025''` in EN





## User

In the oep-results-2026-cleaning.R script, add a new section checking the number of council seats overall by municipality, which should be in concejal_summary. They should be assigned according to census population according to this rule.

En los municipios con hasta 15.000 habitantes se eligen cinco concejales; en aquellos con población de 15.001 a 50.000 habitantes, el número se amplía a siete; mientras que los municipios con 50.001 a 75.000 habitantes eligen nueve concejales. 

En contraste, los municipios capitales de departamento y aquellos que superan los 75.000 habitantes, como Cercado, Sacaba y Quillacollo, cuentan con hasta once concejalas y concejales

You can load census population from data/cpv2025/population_municipality.rds

## Assistant



<div><thinking id="thinking-1781829121730-1inb8">


<span class="thinking-topic">Understanding the task</span>



The user wants to add a new section to `oep-results-2026-cleaning.R` that checks the number of council seats per municipality against the expected number based on census population. 

The rules are:
- ≤ 15,000 inhabitants → 5 concejales
- 15,001 – 50,000 → 7 concejales
- 50,001 – 75,000 → 9 concejales
- Department capitals OR > 75,000 inhabitants → 11 concejales

The population data is at `data/cpv2025/population_municipality.rds`.
</topic>



<span class="thinking-topic">Planning the implementation</span>



I need to load the population data and identify which municipalities are department capitals—the nine capitals in Bolivia are Sucre, La Paz, Cochabamba, Oruro, Potosí, Tarija, Santa Cruz, Trinidad, and Cobija. Then I'll apply the seat assignment rule based on population and capital status, join with the existing `concejal_summary` data to compare expected versus actual seat counts, and flag any discrepancies.

</topic>

Now I need to determine department capitals. In Bolivia, the department capitals are:
- Chuquisaca → Sucre (010101)
- La Paz → La Paz (020101) - actually there are two capitals in Bolivia; Sucre is the constitutional capital, La Paz is the seat of government
- Cochabamba → Cochabamba (030101)
- Oruro → Oruro (040101)
- Potosí → Potosí (050101)
- Tarija → Tarija (060101)
- Santa Cruz → Santa Cruz de la Sierra (070101)
- Beni → Trinidad (080101)
- Pando → Cobija (090101)

So I'm identifying which municipalities are the department capitals based on the rule that these are the municipalities containing the actual capital city. I see the pattern now—the id_muni codes follow a DDPPMMM structure where the first two digits are the department, the next two are the province, and the last three are the municipality. The capital cities all use the XX0101 format, representing the first province and first municipality of each department. 020101 = La Paz, 030101 = Cochabamba, 040101 = Oruro, 050101 = Potosí, 060101 = Tarija, 070101 = Santa Cruz de la Sierra, 080101 = Trinidad, and 090101 = Cobija — I can identify these department capitals by checking if the last two digits of the municipality code equal "01".

I'm considering whether to use a lookup table or just hardcode the nine capital municipality codes directly. The simplest approach would be to generate them programmatically by combining each department number with "0101" to get the capital code.

no census match\n+    ),\n+    sillas_ok = n_sillas == sillas_esperadas\n+  )\n+\n+# Distribution of actual vs expected seat counts\n+concejal_expected |>\n+  count(sillas_esperadas, n_sillas) |>\n+  arrange(sillas_esperadas, n_sillas) |>\n+  print()\n+\n+# Flag anomalies\n+concejal_size_issues <- concejal_expected |>\n+  filter(!isTRUE(sillas_ok)) |>\n+  select(id_muni, municipio, departamento, pop_2024,\n+         es_capital, sillas_esperadas, n_sillas) |>\n+  arrange(departamento, municipio)\n+\n+if (nrow(concejal_size_issues) > 0) {\n+  message(nrow(concejal_size_issues),\n+          \" municipality/ies have unexpected seat counts:\")\n+  print(concejal_size_issues, n = Inf)\n+} else {\n+  message(\"All municipality seat counts match expected values.\")\n+}\n+\n+# ==============================================================================\n # 4. <div><tool-call id="toolu_011qDeQ9r4FaumHPmEjsCumB"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"concejal_size_issues <- concejal_expected |>\n  filter(!isTRUE(sillas_ok)) |>","new_string":"concejal_size_issues <- concejal_expected |>\n  filter(sillas_ok == FALSE | is.na(sillas_ok)) |>"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -302,7 +302,7 @@\n \n # Flag anomalies\n concejal_size_issues <- concejal_expected |>\n-  filter(!isTRUE(sillas_ok)) |>\n+  filter(sillas_ok == FALSE | is.na(sillas_ok)) |>\n   select(id_muni, municipio, departamento, pop_2024,\n          es_capital, sillas_esperadas, n_sillas) |>\n   arrange(departamento, municipio)\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Six anomalies: five municipalities with 6 seats instead of 7, and one (Yapacaní) with 8 instead of 9. These are real issues worth investigating — either the PDF extraction missed a seat, or the OEP data has an irregularity.

The section is working correctly. Here's a summary of the changes:

**`oep-results-2026-cleaning.R`:**

1. **`concejal_summary` group_by** — expanded to `group_by(id_muni, municipio, provincia, departamento)`. Since `id_muni` is 1:1 with `municipio + provincia`, this doesn't change any aggregation — it just makes `id_muni` and `departamento` available in the output for downstream use.

2. **New section 3b** — loads `cpv2024/population_municipality.rds`, identifies the 9 department capitals by their `XX0101` id pattern, applies the electoral seat rule via `case_when`, and joins to `concejal_summary`. Produces `concejal_expected` (the full table with both expected and actual counts) and `concejal_size_issues` (the 6 anomalies). The distribution table is printed regardless for quick review.

The **6 anomalies** found: San Lucas, Achocalla, Cajuata, El Puente, and San Julián each have 6 seats where 7 are expected; Yapacaní has 8 where 9 are expected. These are worth cross-checking against the PDF.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01H4Rvx74N8K76eyAeZM4eU6"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\nconcejal_size_issues |>\n  left_join(\n    muni_concejal_ioc |> distinct(municipio) |> muta...[truncated, total 317 chars]","intent":"Cross-check anomalies against IOC seats to see if they explain the shortfall"},"intent":"Cross-check anomalies against IOC seats to see if they explain the shortfall","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\nconcejal_size_issues |>\n  left_join(\n    muni_concejal_ioc |> distinct(municipio) |> mutate(tiene_ioc = TRUE),\n    by = \"municipio\"\n  ) |>\n  replace_na(list(tiene_ioc = FALSE)) |>\n  mutate(\n    n_sillas_con_ioc = n_sillas + if_else(tiene_ioc, 1L, 0L),\n    resuelto         = n_sillas_con_ioc == sillas_esperadas\n  )\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

All six are IOC municipalities and all six resolve exactly. Now add this cross-check to the cleaning script:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_012chP4qKjEhfyizcKGZuku3"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","old_string":"if (nrow(concejal_size_issues) > 0) {\n  message(nrow(concejal_size_issues),\n          \" mu...[truncated, total 250 chars]","new_string":"if (nrow(concejal_size_issues) > 0) {\n  message(nrow(concejal_size_issues),\n          \" mu...[truncated, total 1072 chars]"},"caption":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","captionLink":"oep-results-2026-cleaning.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -311,6 +311,28 @@\n   message(nrow(concejal_size_issues),\n           \" municipality/ies have unexpected seat counts:\")\n   print(concejal_size_issues, n = Inf)\n+\n+  # Cross-check: municipalities with an IOC seat have one fewer regular seat,\n+  # with the IOC seat filling the gap to reach the expected total.\n+  ioc_check <- concejal_size_issues |>\n+    left_join(\n+      muni_concejal_ioc |> distinct(municipio) |> mutate(tiene_ioc = TRUE),\n+      by = \"municipio\"\n+    ) |>\n+    replace_na(list(tiene_ioc = FALSE)) |>\n+    mutate(\n+      n_sillas_con_ioc = n_sillas + if_else(tiene_ioc, 1L, 0L),\n+      resuelto         = n_sillas_con_ioc == sillas_esperadas\n+    )\n+\n+  n_resuelto    <- sum(ioc_check$resuelto,  na.rm = TRUE)\n+  n_no_resuelto <- sum(!ioc_check$resuelto, na.rm = TRUE)\n+\n+  message(n_resuelto,    \" anomaly/ies resolved by an IOC seat.\")\n+  if (n_no_resuelto > 0) {\n+    message(n_no_resuelto, \" anomaly/ies remain unexplained:\")\n+    print(filter(ioc_check, !resuelto), n = Inf)\n+  }\n } else {\n   message(\"All municipality seat counts match expected values.\")\n }\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_012chP4qKjEhfyizcKGZuku3"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"Updated oep-results-2026-cleaning.R"},"elapsedMs":109}</script></tool-result></div>

All six anomalies are fully explained: every municipality short by one regular seat has a corresponding entry in `muni_concejal_ioc`, and `n_sillas + 1 == sillas_esperadas` in each case. The IOC seat fills the expected total rather than adding to it — these municipalities get 6 regular + 1 IOC = 7 total, or 8 regular + 1 IOC = 9 total.

The added cross-check in the script:
- Joins `concejal_size_issues` against `muni_concejal_ioc` to flag `tiene_ioc`
- Computes `n_sillas_con_ioc` and `resuelto`
- Reports how many anomalies are explained vs. how many genuinely remain unexplained (printing only the unresolved ones, so if everything resolves the output stays clean)

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01M2isyqNEHppkFJ8kGCCv8e"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"# Compose the council intro sentence for a municipality.\n# Returns one sentence for non-IO...[truncated, total 252 chars]","new_string":"# Convert wikitext [[links]] to HTML anchors for in-page display,\n# and strip <ref>...</re...[truncated, total 1778 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -76,6 +76,48 @@\n   )\n }\n \n+# Convert wikitext [[links]] to HTML anchors for in-page display,\n+# and strip <ref>...</ref> tags (which belong only in the copy output).\n+wikilinks_to_html <- function(text) {\n+  # [[Target|Display]] → <a href=\"...\">Display</a>\n+  text <- stringr::str_replace_all(\n+    text,\n+    \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\",\n+    function(m) {\n+      parts <- stringr::str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")\n+      url   <- wikipedia_url_for(parts[, 2])\n+      paste0('<a href=\"', url, '\" target=\"_blank\">', parts[, 3], \"</a>\")\n+    }\n+  )\n+  # [[Target]] → <a href=\"...\">Target</a>\n+  text <- stringr::str_replace_all(\n+    text,\n+    \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\",\n+    function(m) {\n+      target <- stringr::str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")[, 2]\n+      paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(target), '\" target=\"_blank\">', target, \"</a>\")\n+    }\n+  )\n+  # Strip <ref>...</ref>\n+  stringr::str_replace_all(text, \"<ref>.*?</ref>\", \"\")\n+}\n+\n+# Compose the mayor sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n+compose_mayor_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_name, alcaldes_data,\n+                                       page_number) {\n+  row    <- alcaldes_data[alcaldes_data$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n+  nombre <- stringr::str_to_title(row$nombre[1])\n+  ref    <- paste0(\"<ref>\",\n+                   source_ref_with_page_number(page_number, source_ref_en),\n+                   \"</ref>\")\n+  paste0(\n+    \"The Mayor of \", muni_name, \" is \", nombre,\n+    \", who was elected in the \",\n+    \"[[2026 Bolivian regional elections|March 2026 elections]].\",\n+    ref\n+  )\n+}\n+\n # Compose the council intro sentence for a municipality.\n # Returns one sentence for non-IOC municipalities, two for IOC ones.\n compose_council_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_name,\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Everything works. Here's a summary of all changes:

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R`** — new `wikipedia_url_for(title, lang = "en")` that converts a page title to a full Wikipedia URL (spaces → underscores).

**`bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd`:**

- **Setup block**: loads `alcaldes.rds` alongside the existing data objects

- **Helpers block** — two new functions:
  - `wikilinks_to_html(text)`: converts `[[Target|Display]]` → `<a href="...">Display</a>` (using `wikipedia_url_for`) and strips `<ref>...</ref>` for display. Handles both pipe and plain wikilink forms.
  - `compose_mayor_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_name, alcaldes_data, page_number)`: builds the mayor sentence in wikitext, with the `[[2026 Bolivian regional elections|March 2026 elections]]` link and a full `<ref>` citation with page number.

- **`muni_block`**: generates both sentences, combines them into `intro_wt` (the copy payload, two paragraphs separated by `\n\n`). The display shows two `<p>` tags — the mayor paragraph with `wikilinks_to_html()` applied (real clickable link, no `<ref>` clutter), the council paragraph as plain text. One "Copy intro" button captures both.

 - everything else HTML-escaped so wikitext syntax shows literally\n+# Returns list(html = character, refs = character vector of ref contents).\n+process_for_display <- function(text, ref_offset = 0L) {\n+  refs <- character(0)\n+\n+  # Step 1: swap <ref>...</ref> for safe placeholder tokens\n+  processed <- stringr::str_replace_all(text, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\", function(m) {\n+    n    <- length(refs) + 1L\n+    refs <<- c(refs, stringr::str_match(m, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\")[1, 2])\n+    sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n + ref_offset)\n+  })\n+\n+  # Step 2: HTML-escape (turns < > & into block.\n+refs_footnotes <- function(refs, ref_offset = 0L) {\n+  if (length(refs) == 0) return(NULL)\n+  tags$div(\n+    style = \"margin-top:5px; padding-top:4px; border-top:1px solid #e1e4e8;\",\n+ <div><tool-call id="toolu_015ty7QYXWSNER8uf1GCBJqt"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nlibrary(stringr); library(htmltools)\n\n# Inline the key fu...[truncated, total 2040 chars]","intent":"Test ref extraction, sequential numbering, and wikilink conversion"},"intent":"Test ref extraction, sequential numbering, and wikilink conversion","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\nlibrary(stringr); library(htmltools)\n\n# Inline the key functions for testing\nprocess_for_display <- function(text, ref_offset = 0L) {\n  refs <- character(0)\n  processed <- str_replace_all(text, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\", function(m) {\n    n <- length(refs) + 1L\n    refs <<- c(refs, str_match(m, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\")[1, 2])\n    sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n + ref_offset)\n  })\n  escaped <- htmlEscape(processed)\n  escaped <- str_replace_all(escaped, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\", function(m) {\n    p <- str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")\n    paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(p[,2]), '\" target=\"_blank\">', p[,3], \"</a>\")\n  })\n  for (i in seq_along(refs)) {\n    n_abs <- i + ref_offset\n    escaped <- str_replace_all(escaped, sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n_abs),\n                                sprintf('<sup style=\"color:#06c;\">[ref%d]</sup>', n_abs))\n  }\n  list(html = escaped, refs = refs)\n}\n\nsource_ref_en <- \"{{Cite report|title=Resultados|publisher=OEP}}\"\nsource_ref_with_page_number <- function(p, r) paste0(sub(\"\\\\}\\\\}$\",\"\",r),\" | page = \",p,\" }}\")\n\n# Mayor sentence\nmayor_wt <- paste0(\n  \"The Mayor of Sucre is Fátima Elva Tardío Quiroga, who was elected in the \",\n  \"[[2026 Bolivian regional elections|March 2026 elections]].\",\n  \"<ref>\", source_ref_with_page_number(23, source_ref_en), \"</ref>\"\n)\n\n# Council sentence (has its own ref in compose_council_sentence_en)\ncouncil_wt <- paste0(\n  \"Sucre Municipality has 11 council seats, as do all department capitals \",\n  \"and municipalities with populations over 75,000.\",\n  \"<ref>{{Cite news|title=A más población|work=Los Tiempos}}</ref>\"\n)\n\nmayor_disp   <- process_for_display(mayor_wt,   ref_offset = 0L)\ncouncil_disp <- process_for_display(council_wt, ref_offset = length(mayor_disp$refs))\n\ncat(\"Mayor HTML:\\n\", mayor_disp$html, \"\\n\\n\")\ncat(\"Council HTML:\\n\", council_disp$html, \"\\n\\n\")\ncat(\"All refs:\\n\")\nall_refs <- c(mayor_disp$refs, council_disp$refs)\nfor (i in seq_along(all_refs)) cat(sprintf(\"ref%d: %s\\n\", i, all_refs[[i]]))\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Everything works correctly. Here's a summary of all changes to [bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd](bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd):

**Summary section** added at the top (after YAML): a short Markdown block showing the two template structures with variables in `code` formatting — the mayor sentence, the population-conditional council sentence, and the wikitable structure.

**`process_for_display(text, ref_offset)`** — new core display function:
1. Extracts `<ref>…</ref>` blocks, replaces with safe `REFSUP_N_END` placeholders
2. HTML-escapes the remaining text (so wikitext syntax like `|colspan=` shows literally)
3. Converts `[[wikilinks]]` to real anchors via `wikipedia_url_for()`
4. Restores placeholders as blue `<sup>[refN]</sup>` superscripts
5. Returns `list(html, refs)` — the refs are the raw wikitext of each citation

**`refs_footnotes(refs, ref_offset)`** — renders the collected refs as `**refN:** {{...}}` paragraphs in monospace below the display block.

**`wikitext_block`** — updated to use `process_for_display` for the display, append `refs_footnotes`, and continue to use the original raw wikitext for the copy textarea.

**`muni_block`** — updated to process mayor and council sentences with sequential numbering (`ref_offset = length(mayor_disp$refs)` for the second sentence), appending the combined `refs_footnotes` below both paragraphs. The `wikilinks_to_html` function is no longer needed in `muni_block`.





## User

Can we generate Wikitext of the form, "'''Yocalla Municipality''' is the second [[Municipalities of Bolivia|municipal section]] of the [[Tomás Frías Province]] in the [[Potosí Department]] in [[Bolivia]]" from muni_id_lookup_table and prov_id_lookup_table? Use muni_anexo and province_anexo as the preferred text output variables.

id_prov is the first four digits of id_muni.

the ordinal for municipal section is the final two digits of id_muni. Use english::ordinal() to generate that word.

This should appear first in the muni_block, and be copyable separately. Call its section "lead" and the existing mayor and council section "government", as in "Copy lead Wikitext" on the button.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01WgPvDPBb3CNHGyC9tgWDa9"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"# Compose the mayor sentence for a municipality (wikitext).","new_string":"# Compose the Wikipedia lead sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n# Uses muni_anexo and...[truncated, total 809 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -167,6 +167,25 @@\n   )\n }\n \n+# Compose the Wikipedia lead sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n+# Uses muni_anexo and province_anexo as display text; ordinal from last 2 digits\n+# of id_muni via english::ordinal().\n+compose_lead_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_lkp, prov_lkp) {\n+  muni_row <- muni_lkp[muni_lkp$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n+  id_prov  <- substr(id_muni, 1, 4)\n+  prov_row <- prov_lkp[prov_lkp$id_prov == id_prov, ]\n+  muni_num <- as.integer(substr(id_muni, 5, 6))\n+\n+  paste0(\n+    \"'''\", muni_row$muni_anexo, \" Municipality''' is the \",\n+    english::ordinal(muni_num),\n+    \" [[Municipalities of Bolivia|municipal section]] of the\",\n+    \" [[\", prov_row$province_anexo, \" Province]]\",\n+    \" in the [[\", prov_row$department, \" Department]]\",\n+    \" in [[Bolivia]].\"\n+  )\n+}\n+\n # Compose the mayor sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n compose_mayor_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_name, alcaldes_data,\n                                        page_number) {\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Tbq4RxatZrzsmGx53Ln1SF"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\n# Redefine process_for_display to match the current QMD definition\nprocess_for_display <-...[truncated, total 1369 chars]","intent":"Confirm lead sentence fully converts all wikilinks to anchors"},"intent":"Confirm lead sentence fully converts all wikilinks to anchors","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\n# Redefine process_for_display to match the current QMD definition\nprocess_for_display <- function(text, ref_offset = 0L) {\n  refs <- character(0)\n  processed <- str_replace_all(text, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\", function(m) {\n    n <- length(refs) + 1L\n    refs <<- c(refs, str_match(m, \"<ref>(.*?)</ref>\")[1, 2])\n    sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n + ref_offset)\n  })\n  escaped <- htmltools::htmlEscape(processed)\n  escaped <- str_replace_all(escaped, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\", function(m) {\n    p <- str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^|\\\\]]+)\\\\|([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")\n    paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(p[,2]), '\" target=\"_blank\">', p[,3], \"</a>\")\n  })\n  escaped <- str_replace_all(escaped, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\", function(m) {\n    tgt <- str_match(m, \"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]]+)\\\\]\\\\]\")[,2]\n    paste0('<a href=\"', wikipedia_url_for(tgt), '\" target=\"_blank\">', tgt, \"</a>\")\n  })\n  for (i in seq_along(refs)) {\n    n_abs <- i + ref_offset\n    escaped <- str_replace_all(escaped, sprintf(\"REFSUP_%d_END\", n_abs),\n                                sprintf('<sup style=\"color:#06c;font-size:9px;\">[ref%d]</sup>', n_abs))\n  }\n  list(html = escaped, refs = refs)\n}\n\n# Full lead sentence through process_for_display\nlead <- compose_lead_sentence_en(\"050901\", muni_id_lookup_table, prov_id_lookup_table)\ndisp <- process_for_display(lead)\ncat(\"Wikitext:\\n\", lead, \"\\n\\nHTML:\\n\", disp$html, \"\\n\")\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Provinces with three or more municipalities won't get special sentences. passes the result through `process_for_display`. The additional sentence contains `[[wikilinks]]` that will be converted to HTML anchors automatically.\n\n## Notes\n\n- `muni_province_context` is computed once in setup and referenced globally by the helper function (same pattern as `concejal_expected`).\n- The `id_prov` column must be added to `muni_id_lookup_table` in setup — it was added to the session variable earlier in this conversation but may not persist after a fresh QMD render.\n- `area_muni` covers 339 municipalities; any missing values will produce `NA` area rank and the context sentence will be skipped (returns NULL from the n==2 branch implicitly via NA conditions). A warning check can be added if needed.\n- The 2-municipality sentence uses the province link as `[[YYY Province]]`, which `process_for_display` will render as an HTML anchor — consistent with existing wikilink handling.\n","language":"markdown"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Bo8DiAH6YKs74YeHpbXCAo"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"concejal_expected <- concejal_summary |>","new_string":"# Municipality areas from admin3 boundary file\narea_muni <- readxl::read_excel(here::here(...[truncated, total 964 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -60,6 +60,30 @@\n   select(id_muni = ine_code, pop_2024 = pop_2024_total)\n dept_capital_ids <- paste0(sprintf(\"%02d\", 1:9), \"0101\")\n \n+# Municipality areas from admin3 boundary file\n+area_muni <- readxl::read_excel(here::here(\"maps\", \"bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\"),\n+                                  sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n+  mutate(id_muni = stringr::str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n+  select(id_muni, area_sqkm)\n+\n+# Add id_prov if not already present, then build province-context lookup\n+muni_lookup <- muni_lookup |>\n+  mutate(id_prov = stringr::str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4))\n+\n+prov_counts <- muni_lookup |> dplyr::count(id_prov, name = \"n_prov_munis\")\n+\n+muni_province_context <- muni_lookup |>\n+  select(id_muni, id_prov, muni_anexo) |>\n+  left_join(prov_counts, by = \"id_prov\") |>\n+  left_join(pop_muni,    by = \"id_muni\") |>\n+  left_join(area_muni,   by = \"id_muni\") |>\n+  group_by(id_prov) |>\n+  mutate(\n+    area_rank = rank(-area_sqkm, ties.method = \"first\"),  # 1 = largest\n+    pop_rank  = rank(-pop_2024,  ties.method = \"first\")   # 1 = most populous\n+  ) |>\n+  ungroup()\n+\n concejal_expected <- concejal_summary |>\n   left_join(pop_muni, by = \"id_muni\") |>\n   mutate(\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01VEjgGjte2DFp8wHnrx9N2r"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"# Compose the Wikipedia lead sentence for a municipality (wikitext).","new_string":"# Compose the province-context sentence appended to the lead, for municipalities\n# in 1- o...[truncated, total 1538 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -191,6 +191,37 @@\n   )\n }\n \n+# Compose the province-context sentence appended to the lead, for municipalities\n+# in 1- or 2-municipality provinces. Returns NULL for 3+ municipality provinces.\n+compose_province_context_en <- function(id_muni, muni_pc, prov_lkp) {\n+  row       <- muni_pc[muni_pc$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n+  id_prov   <- stringr::str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4)\n+  prov_name <- prov_lkp$province_anexo[prov_lkp$id_prov == id_prov]\n+  muni_name <- row$muni_anexo\n+  prov_link <- paste0(\"[[\", prov_name, \" Province]]\")\n+  n         <- row$n_prov_munis\n+\n+  if (n == 1) {\n+    return(paste0(\n+      muni_name, \" Municipality is the only municipality in \",\n+      prov_link, \" and its boundaries are identical.\"\n+    ))\n+  }\n+\n+  if (n == 2 && !is.na(row$area_rank) && !is.na(row$pop_rank)) {\n+    larger   <- row$area_rank == 1\n+    more_pop <- row$pop_rank  == 1\n+    return(dplyr::case_when(\n+       larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the larger and more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\"),\n+       larger & !more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality covers the larger area of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it is less populous.\"),\n+      !larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it covers a smaller area.\"),\n+      TRUE                ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the smaller and less populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\")\n+    ))\n+  }\n+\n+  NULL\n+}\n+\n # Compose the Wikipedia lead sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n # Uses muni_anexo and province_anexo as display text; ordinal from last 2 digits\n # of id_muni via english::ordinal().\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01Sf8msUh1MruNdBq6TQMDeZ"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"  paste0(\n    \"'''\", muni_row$muni_anexo, \" Municipality''' is the \",\n    english::ordinal...[truncated, total 300 chars]","new_string":"  lead <- paste0(\n    \"'''\", muni_row$muni_anexo, \" Municipality''' is the \",\n    english:...[truncated, total 448 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -231,7 +231,7 @@\n   prov_row <- prov_lkp[prov_lkp$id_prov == id_prov, ]\n   muni_num <- as.integer(substr(id_muni, 5, 6))\n \n-  paste0(\n+  lead <- paste0(\n     \"'''\", muni_row$muni_anexo, \" Municipality''' is the \",\n     english::ordinal(muni_num),\n     \" [[Municipalities of Bolivia|municipal section]] of the\",\n@@ -239,6 +239,9 @@\n     \" in the [[\", prov_row$department, \" Department]]\",\n     \" in [[Bolivia]].\"\n   )\n+\n+  context <- compose_province_context_en(id_muni, muni_province_context, prov_lkp)\n+  if (!is.null(context)) paste(lead, context) else lead\n }\n \n # Compose the mayor sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01AsrNQJ47eaDvTrm7cgYGoR"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\nlibrary(readxl); library(dplyr); library(stringr); library(english)\nsource(\"src/wikipedia...[truncated, total 1689 chars]","intent":"Verify province counts and find one example of each sentence case"},"intent":"Verify province counts and find one example of each sentence case","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\nlibrary(readxl); library(dplyr); library(stringr); library(english)\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\n\n# Build the same data the QMD setup will build\narea_muni <- read_excel(here::here(\"maps\", \"bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx\"), sheet = \"bol_admin3\") |>\n  mutate(id_muni = str_sub(adm3_pcode, 3, 8)) |>\n  select(id_muni, area_sqkm)\n\nmuni_lookup2 <- muni_id_lookup_table |>\n  mutate(id_prov = str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4))\nprov_counts <- muni_lookup2 |> count(id_prov, name = \"n_prov_munis\")\npop_muni2   <- readRDS(here::here(\"data\", \"cpv2024\", \"population_municipality.rds\")) |>\n  select(id_muni = ine_code, pop_2024 = pop_2024_total)\n\nmuni_province_context <- muni_lookup2 |>\n  select(id_muni, id_prov, muni_anexo) |>\n  left_join(prov_counts, by = \"id_prov\") |>\n  left_join(pop_muni2,   by = \"id_muni\") |>\n  left_join(area_muni,   by = \"id_muni\") |>\n  group_by(id_prov) |>\n  mutate(area_rank = rank(-area_sqkm, ties.method = \"first\"),\n         pop_rank  = rank(-pop_2024,  ties.method = \"first\")) |>\n  ungroup()\n\n# Spot-check province counts\ncat(\"1-muni provinces:\", sum(prov_counts$n_prov_munis == 1), \"\\n\")\ncat(\"2-muni provinces:\", sum(prov_counts$n_prov_munis == 2), \"\\n\")\n\n# Find examples of each 2-muni case\ncases <- muni_province_context |>\n  filter(n_prov_munis == 2) |>\n  mutate(case = case_when(\n    area_rank == 1 &  pop_rank == 1 ~ \"larger+more_pop\",\n    area_rank == 1 &  pop_rank == 2 ~ \"larger+less_pop\",\n    area_rank == 2 &  pop_rank == 1 ~ \"smaller+more_pop\",\n    area_rank == 2 &  pop_rank == 2 ~ \"smaller+less_pop\"\n  )) |>\n  group_by(case) |> slice(1)\n\ncat(\"\\nExample municipalities for each 2-muni case:\\n\")\ncases |> select(id_muni, muni_anexo, id_prov, area_sqkm, pop_2024, case)\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

more_pop <- row$pop_rank == 1\n    return(case_when(\n       larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the larger and more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\"),\n       larger & !more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality covers the larger area of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it is less populous.\"),\n      !larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it covers a smaller area.\"),\n      TRUE                ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the smaller and less populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\")\n    ))\n  }\n  NULL\n}\n\n# Test all four 2-muni cases + one 1-muni case\ntest_ids <- list(\n  \"1-muni solo\"       = cases$id_muni[1],   # 2-muni for now; find a real 1-muni\n  \"larger+more_pop\"   = \"010501\",\n  \"larger+less_pop\"   = \"010201\",\n  \"smaller+more_pop\"  = \"010202\",\n  \"smaller+less_pop\"  = \"010502\"\n)\n# Find a genuine 1-muni province\none_muni_id <- muni_province_context |> filter(n_prov_munis == 1) |> slice(1) |> pull(id_muni)\ntest_ids[[\"1-muni\"]] <- one_muni_id\n\nfor (label in names(test_ids)) {\n  mid <- test_ids[[label]]\n  s   <- compose_province_context_en(mid, muni_province_context, prov_id_lookup_table)\n  cat(sprintf(\"[%s] %s\\n    → %s\\n\\n\", label, mid, s))\n}\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

All five cases produce correct output. Here's a summary of everything added:

**`bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd` — setup block:**
- Loads `maps/bol_admin_boundaries.xlsx` → sheet `bol_admin3`, extracts `id_muni` from `adm3_pcode` chars 3–8, selects `area_sqkm`
- Ensures `muni_lookup` has `id_prov`, computes `prov_counts`
- Builds `muni_province_context`: joins municipality list → province counts → population → area, then within each province computes `area_rank` and `pop_rank` (1 = largest/most populous)

**`bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd` — helpers block:**
- New `compose_province_context_en(id_muni, muni_pc, prov_lkp)`:
  - `n == 1`: identical-boundaries sentence
  - `n == 2`: one of four `case_when` sentences based on `area_rank`/`pop_rank`
  - `n > 2` or missing data: returns `NULL` (no sentence added)

**`compose_lead_sentence_en`** — now calls `compose_province_context_en` and pastes the result after the main lead sentence when non-NULL. The added sentence contains `[[Province]]` wikilinks, which `process_for_display` already handles by converting them to HTML anchors in the display and leaving them as wikitext in the copy buffer.

There are 15 single-municipality provinces and 35 two-municipality provinces, covering 50 + 70 = 120 municipalities that get a context sentence.





## User

Add a  sentence just prior to that one that appears if a municipality is the most populous or second most populous in the country or its department. If it is not, do the same for most populous in its province (if the province has 3 or more municipalities in it).

## Assistant



<div><thinking id="thinking-1781838375971-bxk09">


<span class="thinking-topic">Understanding the ranking hierarchy</span>



The user wants a sentence about population ranking that:

1. **National rank**: If municipality is #1 or #2 most populous in Bolivia → sentence about national ranking
2. **Department rank**: If not national top-2, but is #1 or #2 most populous in its department → sentence about department ranking  
3. **Province rank**: If not qualifying for national or department, AND province has 3+ municipalities → sentence if municipality is the most populous in its province

So the priority is:
1. Check national rank (top 2) → national sentence
2. Otherwise check department rank (top 2) → department sentence
3. Otherwise check province rank (most populous, only if province has 3+ municipalities) → province sentence
4. Otherwise no sentence

This ranking sentence appears before the province-context sentence in the lead text, so the order will be: lead sentence, then population rank sentence if applicable, then province context sentence if applicable.



<span class="thinking-topic">Designing the sentence templates</span>



Now I'm working on the sentence templates for each ranking level, starting with the national rank case. I need to compute population ranks at three levels: national, department, and province. I'll extend the existing `muni_province_context` table to include these ranks, then use the province lookup to get department information for the department-level ranking. Adding ranking columns... Adding ranking columns... Writing function logic...

I'm verifying that the `pop_rank` column correctly represents the rank within each province, and confirming the filter for `n_prov_munis >= 3` properly identifies provinces with enough municipalities to warrant the context statement.

For the wording, I'm leaning toward using just the municipality name in these declarative statements rather than repeating "Municipality" each time. <div><tool-call id="toolu_01QZTrz53axYsFgSPn1T5psb"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","old_string":"# Compose the province-context sentence appended to the lead, for municipalities\n# in 1- or 2-municipality provinces.","new_string":"# Compose a population-rank sentence for municipalities that are among the most\n# populous...[truncated, total 1398 chars]"},"caption":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","captionLink":"bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -196,6 +196,36 @@\n   )\n }\n \n+# Compose a population-rank sentence for municipalities that are among the most\n+# populous nationally (top 2), in their department (top 2), or in their province\n+# (rank 1, only for provinces with 3+ municipalities). Returns NULL when none apply.\n+compose_pop_rank_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_pc) {\n+  row <- muni_pc[muni_pc$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n+\n+  # National top-2\n+  if (!is.na(row$nat_pop_rank) && row$nat_pop_rank <= 2) {\n+    ord <- if (row$nat_pop_rank == 1) \"most\" else \"second most\"\n+    return(paste0(\"It is the \", ord, \" populous municipality in [[Bolivia]].\"))\n+  }\n+\n+  # Department top-2\n+  if (!is.na(row$dept_pop_rank) && row$dept_pop_rank <= 2) {\n+    ord      <- if (row$dept_pop_rank == 1) \"most\" else \"second most\"\n+    dept_lnk <- paste0(\"[[\", row$department, \" Department]]\")\n+    return(paste0(\"It is the \", ord, \" populous municipality in \", dept_lnk, \".\"))\n+  }\n+\n+  # Province most populous (only for provinces with 3+ municipalities)\n+  if (!is.na(row$pop_rank) && row$pop_rank == 1 && row$n_prov_munis >= 3) {\n+    id_prov   <- stringr::str_sub(id_muni, 1, 4)\n+    prov_name <- prov_lookup$province_anexo[prov_lookup$id_prov == id_prov]\n+    return(paste0(\"It is the most populous municipality in [[\",\n+                  prov_name, \" Province]].\"))\n+  }\n+\n+  NULL\n+}\n+\n # Compose the province-context sentence appended to the lead, for municipalities\n # in 1- or 2-municipality provinces. Returns NULL for 3+ municipality provinces.\n compose_province_context_en <- function(id_muni, muni_pc, prov_lkp) {\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Summary of changes to [bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd](bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd):

**Setup block** — extended `muni_province_context` build:
- Joins `prov_lookup` to bring in `department` names
- `mutate(nat_pop_rank = rank(-pop_2024, ...))` across all rows for national ranking
- `group_by(department) |> mutate(dept_pop_rank = ...)` for department ranking

**Helpers block** — new `compose_pop_rank_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_pc)`:
1. National top-2 check → "It is the [most / second most] populous municipality in [[Bolivia]]."
2. Department top-2 check → "... in [[X Department]]."
3. Province rank-1 check (only if `n_prov_munis >= 3`) → "... in [[X Province]]."
4. Otherwise → `NULL`

**`compose_lead_sentence_en`** — assembles all three sentence pieces with `NULL` filtering:
```r
parts <- c(lead, pop_rank, context)
paste(parts[!sapply(parts, is.null)], collapse = " ")
```

The new sentence sits between the opening "is the Nth municipal section..." sentence and the province-context comparative sentence. All `[[wikilinks]]` it generates pass through `process_for_display` to become HTML anchors in the preview.

NULL\n}\n\nsource(\"src/wikipedia-tools.R\")\n\n# Test: Santa Cruz (national #1), Sucre (dept #1), Villa Zudáñez (prov #1)\nfor (mid in c(\"070101\", \"010101\", \"010301\")) {\n  wt   <- <div><tool-call id="toolu_01UhBUkgFjr9j6WSaho7v1of"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\n# Check auto_muni for 2012 columns and total structure\nauto_muni |> select(ine_code, tota...[truncated, total 586 chars]","intent":"Find total denominators for both language and ethnicity tables"},"intent":"Find total denominators for both language and ethnicity tables","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\n# Check auto_muni for 2012 columns and total structure\nauto_muni |> select(ine_code, total, ends_with(\"2012\")) |> head(3) |> glimpse()\n\n# Also check what total denominator looks like for idiomas\nidiomas_hablados_muni |> \n  select(ine_code, matches(\"total|poblacion|pop\")) |> \n  head(3)\n\n# Check what the language file totals look like by summing a known row\nrow1 <- idiomas_hablados_muni |> filter(ine_code == \"010101\")\ncat(\"\\nSum of 2024 indigenous language columns for Sucre:\\n\")\nrow1 |> select(ends_with(\"2024\")) |> unlist() |> sum()\ncat(\"\\ncastellano_2024:\", row1$castellano_2024)\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_012tYgsPjEyyMELbBgzeVxgo"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"\n# idiomas doesn't have a totals column — need to check what the denominator is\n# Let's lo...[truncated, total 376 chars]","intent":"Check if autoidentification has 2012 data"},"intent":"Check if autoidentification has 2012 data","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"\n# idiomas doesn't have a totals column — need to check what the denominator is\n# Let's look at the 2012 language data to understand what \"total\" means\n# The sum 406379 > pop 296746, so it counts languages spoken (multiple per person)\n\n# Check autoidentification: does it have 2012 data?\nauto_muni |> select(ine_code, total, starts_with(\"total\"), contains(\"2012\")) |> names()\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<span class="thinking-topic">Understanding what data is available</span>



Now I have a clear picture:

**Language data (`idiomas_hablados_muni`):**
- Has both 2012 and 2024 columns for each language
- Non-_ext_ columns are Bolivian/indigenous languages (plus castellano)
- _ext_ columns are foreign languages
- No total denominator column — need to use `pop` table (`pop_2012_total`, `pop_2024_total`)
- Since multiple languages can be spoken per person, the denominator might be total speakers, not total population
- For the language use table, I should probably use total population as the denominator to show what percentage of the population speaks each language

**Autoidentification data (`auto_muni`):**
- Only has 2024 data, no 2012 columns
- Includes a `total` column for people who self-identified as indigenous
- This creates a mismatch with the user's request for both years, though they might be okay with just 2024 for autoidentification while using 2012 for language data

I'm checking if there's a 2012 autoidentification dataset available elsewhere, though the municipality-level file the user requested only contains 2024. For the wikitables approach, I'll show both years for language data but may need to use only 2024 for autoidentification unless I find historical data. I'll structure the language table with columns for both years where available, and the autoidentification table with just 2024 since that's all we have. Let me build out the wikitable format with the speaker counts and percentages for each language and identity category. So the denominator for autoidentification percentages should be the total population, not just those who self-identified, since I'm trying to show what proportion of the overall population identifies as indigenous. For individual groups, I can calculate both ways — as a percentage of total population for broad comparison, or as a percentage of self-identified indigenous people for within-group proportions. The total population approach seems clearer for consistency across both language and autoidentification tables. I'm also realizing that for languages, people might report multiple languages, so the denominator could exceed total population.

Now I'm thinking through the function structure: one function to build the languages table that filters to native Bolivian languages, calculates percentages for both years, and includes only languages at 1% or higher; another function for autoidentification that shows groups at 1% of total population, sorted by count, though this only has 2024 data available. The user asked for both years, but autoidentification data is only available for 2024, so I need to handle that limitation.

For displaying the language names, I'll strip the year suffix and convert to title case—so `castellano_2024` becomes `Castellano`, and compound names like `machajuyay_kallawaya` become `Machajuyay Kallawaya`. These tables should go into the municipality block as a separate section since the user requested two WikiTables per municipality without specifying exact placement.

My approach is to load both data files upfront, then create helper functions that generate both the WikiTable markup and preview kables for each table type. I'll add two new sections to the municipality block—one for identity demographics and one for languages—each with its own copy button.

For the English output, I'm using "Ethnic identification" and "Languages spoken" as the table titles, and structuring the WikiTables with appropriate headers and data rows for the census information.

Generating the table markup... For the 2012 data, I'll apply the same denominator approach using the total population from that year. When displaying the names, I'm stripping the year suffixes and converting to title case, with a special mapping for "castellano" to "Spanish" for the English Wikipedia context. For the 1% threshold, I'll include any group or language that meets or exceeds 1% in either 2024 or 2012, rather than requiring it in both years.

Now I'm setting up the implementation by placing the wikitable functions in `src/wikipedia-tools.R` for consistency with the existing council table builders, since they'll be general functions that accept data as parameters rather than relying on global objects.

Writing the table generation functions... Writing the data processing code... Writing the output functions... Writing helper functions... Generating the wikitable...

I realize I need to include `pop_2012_total` in the data load, not just `pop_2024_total`, so I can calculate the percentage comparisons between years. Now I'm planning the implementation structure: I'll load the additional demographic datasets in the QMD setup, add helper functions to the tools file, then insert two new display sections in the municipality block—one for ethnic identification and one for languages, each showing the R table alongside its Wikipedia equivalent. For the auto data which only has 2024 values, I'll create a simplified wikitable with just that single year's column.

The ethnic group columns in the auto dataset are already properly formatted with special characters and title case, so I mainly need to handle the pivoting carefully to filter out the metadata columns and apply the 1% threshold to reduce the many sparse ethnic categories. I'll add these functions at the end of the Bolivia section in the tools file.

Writing the implementation code... I need to be careful about how `pop_2012` flows through the data pipeline—when I update `pop_muni` to include it, the join in `muni_province_context` will automatically pick it up, but that's fine since the ranking only uses `pop_2024`. For the wikitable captions, I'm thinking "Languages spoken, 2012 and 2024 censuses" for the language table and "Ethnic identification, 2024 census" for the auto-identification table, and both should cite the same INE census source.

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01SEQNYbE1BUrtw4ZBK9zvxW"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"# Backward-compatible aliases\nbuild_council_wikitable <- build_council_wikitable_es\nbuild_...[truncated, total 133 chars]","new_string":"# Backward-compatible aliases\nbuild_council_wikitable <- build_council_wikitable_es\nbuild_...[truncated, total 7695 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -376,6 +376,194 @@\n build_council_wikitable <- build_council_wikitable_es\n build_council_kable     <- build_council_kable_en\n \n+# ---- Bolivia demographic tables (language use & ethnic identification) --------\n+\n+# Internal helpers\n+.fmt_n   <- function(x) format(round(x), big.mark = \",\", scientific = FALSE)\n+.fmt_pct <- function(x) sprintf(\"%.1f%%\", x * 100)\n+\n+.clean_lang_name <- function(x) {\n+  x |>\n+    stringr::str_remove(\"_(2024|2012)$\") |>\n+    stringr::str_replace_all(\"_\", \" \") |>\n+    stringr::str_to_title() |>\n+    stringr::str_replace(\"^Castellano$\", \"Spanish\") |>\n+    stringr::str_replace(\"^Otras Declaraciones$\", \"Other\")\n+}\n+\n+# Internal: append optional source row spanning ncols columns\n+.append_source_row <- function(lines, ncols, source, source_refs, source_label) {\n+  if (!is.null(source)) {\n+    src_text <- paste0(\"'''\", source_label, \":''' \", source)\n+    if (!is.null(source_refs))\n+      src_text <- paste0(src_text, \"<ref>\", source_refs, \"</ref>\")\n+    lines <- c(lines, \"|-\", paste0('|colspan=\"', ncols, '\"|', src_text))\n+  }\n+  lines\n+}\n+\n+#' Build a language-use wikitable for a municipality (English)\n+#'\n+#' Shows each indigenous/national language spoken by ≥ 1% of the population\n+#' in 2024 or 2012, with counts and percentages for both censuses.\n+#' Denominator: total resident population (not unique speakers).\n+#'\n+#' @param id_muni     Six-digit id_muni / ine_code string\n+#' @param lang_data   `idiomas_hablados_muni` data frame\n+#' @param pop_data    Data frame with columns id_muni, pop_2024, pop_2012\n+#' @param caption     Optional wikitable caption\n+#' @param source,source_refs  Optional source row (same pattern as get_wikitable)\n+build_language_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data,\n+                                         caption = \"Languages spoken\",\n+                                         source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n+  row      <- lang_data[lang_data$ine_code == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_row  <- pop_data[pop_data$id_muni   == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_2024 <- pop_row$pop_2024\n+  pop_2012 <- pop_row$pop_2012\n+\n+  # Native + national language columns only (exclude _ext_ foreign languages)\n+  cols_2024 <- names(lang_data) |>\n+    stringr::str_subset(\"_2024$\") |>\n+    stringr::str_subset(\"_ext_\", negate = TRUE)\n+  cols_2012 <- stringr::str_replace(cols_2024, \"_2024$\", \"_2012\")\n+  # Keep only pairs that exist in 2012 as well\n+  valid     <- cols_2012 %in% names(lang_data)\n+  cols_2024 <- cols_2024[valid]; cols_2012 <- cols_2012[valid]\n+\n+  langs <- tibble::tibble(\n+    language = .clean_lang_name(cols_2024),\n+    n_2024   = as.numeric(row[cols_2024]),\n+    n_2012   = as.numeric(row[cols_2012]),\n+    pct_2024 = n_2024 / pop_2024,\n+    pct_2012 = n_2012 / pop_2012\n+  ) |>\n+    dplyr::filter(pct_2024 >= 0.01 | pct_2012 >= 0.01) |>\n+    dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(n_2024))\n+\n+  if (nrow(langs) == 0) return(\"\")\n+\n+  lines <- c('{| class=\"wikitable\"')\n+  if (!is.null(caption)) lines <- c(lines, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n+  lines <- c(lines,\n+    \"! Language !! 2024 speakers !! 2024 % !! 2012 speakers !! 2012 %\")\n+\n+  for (i in seq_len(nrow(langs))) {\n+    r <- langs[i, ]\n+    lines <- c(lines, \"|-\",\n+               sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s || %s || %s\",\n+                       r$language,\n+                       .fmt_n(r$n_2024), .fmt_pct(r$pct_2024),\n+                       .fmt_n(r$n_2012), .fmt_pct(r$pct_2012)))\n+  }\n+\n+  lines <- .append_source_row(lines, 5L, source, source_refs, \"Source\")\n+  lines <- c(lines, \"|}\")\n+  paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n+}\n+\n+#' Build an ethnic-identification wikitable for a municipality (English, 2024 only)\n+#'\n+#' Shows each group self-identified by ≥ 1% of the total population.\n+#' Denominator: total resident population.\n+#'\n+#' @param id_muni    Six-digit id_muni / ine_code string\n+#' @param auto_data  `autoidentificacion_muni` data frame\n+#' @param pop_data   Data frame with columns id_muni, pop_2024\n+#' @param caption    Optional wikitable caption\n+#' @param source,source_refs  Optional source row\n+build_autoident_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, auto_data, pop_data,\n+                                          caption = \"Ethnic identification (2024)\",\n+                                          source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n+  row      <- auto_data[auto_data$ine_code == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_2024 <- pop_data$pop_2024[pop_data$id_muni == id_muni]\n+\n+  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n+                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\",\n+                 # English variants\n+                 \"municipality\", \"province\", \"department\", \"level\")\n+\n+  groups <- tibble::tibble(\n+    group = names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols],\n+    n     = as.numeric(row[names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols]])\n+  ) |>\n+    dplyr::mutate(pct = n / pop_2024) |>\n+    dplyr::filter(pct >= 0.01) |>\n+    dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(n))\n+\n+  if (nrow(groups) == 0) return(\"\")\n+\n+  lines <- c('{| class=\"wikitable\"')\n+  if (!is.null(caption)) lines <- c(lines, paste0(\"|+ \", caption))\n+  lines <- c(lines, \"! Group !! People !! %\")\n+\n+  for (i in seq_len(nrow(groups))) {\n+    r <- groups[i, ]\n+    lines <- c(lines, \"|-\",\n+               sprintf(\"| %s || %s || %s\",\n+                       r$group, .fmt_n(r$n), .fmt_pct(r$pct)))\n+  }\n+\n+  lines <- .append_source_row(lines, 3L, source, source_refs, \"Source\")\n+  lines <- c(lines, \"|}\")\n+  paste(lines, collapse = \"\\n\")\n+}\n+\n+#' HTML kable preview of language use\n+build_language_kable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data) {\n+  row      <- lang_data[lang_data$ine_code == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_row  <- pop_data[pop_data$id_muni   == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_2024 <- pop_row$pop_2024; pop_2012 <- pop_row$pop_2012\n+\n+  cols_2024 <- names(lang_data) |>\n+    stringr::str_subset(\"_2024$\") |>\n+    stringr::str_subset(\"_ext_\", negate = TRUE)\n+  cols_2012 <- stringr::str_replace(cols_2024, \"_2024$\", \"_2012\")\n+  valid     <- cols_2012 %in% names(lang_data)\n+  cols_2024 <- cols_2024[valid]; cols_2012 <- cols_2012[valid]\n+\n+  df <- tibble::tibble(\n+    Language        = .clean_lang_name(cols_2024),\n+    `2024 speakers` = as.numeric(row[cols_2024]),\n+    `2024 %`        = sprintf(\"%.1f%%\", as.numeric(row[cols_2024]) / pop_2024 * 100),\n+    `2012 speakers` = as.numeric(row[cols_2012]),\n+    `2012 %`        = sprintf(\"%.1f%%\", as.numeric(row[cols_2012]) / pop_2012 * 100)\n+  ) |>\n+    dplyr::filter(as.numeric(`2024 speakers`) / pop_2024 >= 0.01 |\n+                  as.numeric(`2012 speakers`) / pop_2012 >= 0.01) |>\n+    dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(`2024 speakers`)) |>\n+    dplyr::mutate(`2024 speakers` = .fmt_n(`2024 speakers`),\n+                  `2012 speakers` = .fmt_n(`2012 speakers`))\n+\n+  htmltools::HTML(\n+    knitr::kable(df, format = \"html\", escape = TRUE,\n+                 table.attr = 'class=\"table table-sm table-striped\" style=\"font-size:11px;width:100%;\"')\n+  )\n+}\n+\n+#' HTML kable preview of ethnic identification\n+build_autoident_kable_en <- function(id_muni, auto_data, pop_data) {\n+  row      <- auto_data[auto_data$ine_code == id_muni, ]\n+  pop_2024 <- pop_data$pop_2024[pop_data$id_muni == id_muni]\n+\n+  meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n+                 \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\",\n+                 \"municipality\", \"province\", \"department\", \"level\")\n+\n+  df <- tibble::tibble(\n+    Group   = names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols],\n+    People  = as.numeric(row[names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols]])\n+  ) |>\n+    dplyr::mutate(`%` = sprintf(\"%.1f%%\", People / pop_2024 * 100)) |>\n+    dplyr::filter(People / pop_2024 >= 0.01) |>\n+    dplyr::arrange(dplyr::desc(People)) |>\n+    dplyr::mutate(People = .fmt_n(People))\n+\n+  htmltools::HTML(\n+    knitr::kable(df, format = \"html\", escape = TRUE,\n+                 table.attr = 'class=\"table table-sm table-striped\" style=\"font-size:11px;width:100%;\"')\n+  )\n+}\n+\n # ---- MediaWiki table formatter (from import-ice-detention.qmd) ----------------\n \n #' Format a data frame as a MediaWiki wikitable string\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>
