<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01SB43jivprRr1oQt1fZ59tb"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 19.6KB, 523 lines, showing lines 300-349 (truncated)]\n   300→    more_pop <- row$po...[truncated, total 2708 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 19.6KB, 523 lines, showing lines 300-349 (truncated)]\n    more_pop <- row$pop_rank  == 1\n    return(dplyr::case_when(\n       larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the larger and more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\"),\n       larger & !more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality covers the larger area of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it is less populous.\"),\n      !larger &  more_pop ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the more populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \", but it covers a smaller area.\"),\n      TRUE                ~ paste0(muni_name, \" Municipality is the smaller and less populous of the two municipalities in \", prov_link, \".\")\n    ))\n  }\n\n  NULL\n}\n\n# Compose the Wikipedia lead sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\n# Uses muni_anexo and province_anexo as display text; ordinal from last 2 digits\n# of id_muni via english::ordinal().\ncompose_lead_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_lkp, prov_lkp) {\n  muni_row <- muni_lkp[muni_lkp$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n  id_prov  <- substr(id_muni, 1, 4)\n  prov_row <- prov_lkp[prov_lkp$id_prov == id_prov, ]\n  muni_num <- as.integer(substr(id_muni, 5, 6))\n\n  lead <- paste0(\n    \"'''\", muni_row$muni_anexo, \" Municipality''' is the \",\n    english::ordinal(muni_num),\n    \" [[Municipalities of Bolivia|municipal section]] of the\",\n    \" [[\", prov_row$province_anexo, \" Province]]\",\n    \" in the [[\", prov_row$department, \" Department]]\",\n    \" in [[Bolivia]].\"\n  )\n\n  pop_rank <- compose_pop_rank_sentence_en(id_muni, muni_province_context)\n  context  <- compose_province_context_en(id_muni, muni_province_context, prov_lkp)\n\n  parts <- c(lead, pop_rank, context)\n  paste(parts[!sapply(parts, is.null)], collapse = \" \")\n}\n\n# Compose the mayor sentence for a municipality (wikitext).\ncompose_mayor_sentence_en <- function(id_muni, muni_name, alcaldes_data,\n                                       page_number) {\n  row    <- alcaldes_data[alcaldes_data$id_muni == id_muni, ]\n  nombre <- stringr::str_to_title(row$nombre[1])\n  ref    <- paste0(\"<ref>\",\n                   source_ref_with_page_number(page_number, source_ref_en),\n                   \"</ref>\")\n  paste0(\n    \"The Mayor of \", muni_name, \" is \", nombre,\n    \", who was elected in the \",\n    \"[[2026 Bolivian regional elections|March 2026 elections]].\",\n    ref"},"elapsedMs":64}</script></tool-result></div>

margin:4px 0 0; color:#333;\"),\n        tags$textarea(\n          id       = paste0(\"lead_en_\", id_muni),\n          style    = \"position:absolute; left:-9999px; height:0; width:0; overflow:hidden;\",\n          readonly = TRUE,\n          lead_wt\n        )\n      ),\n      # Government section — mayor + council intro, one button copies both\n      tags$div(\n        style = \"margin-top:8px; <div><tool-call id="toolu_01AsXPPC4U38ziYHMTBiXqUB"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"build_autoident_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, auto_data, pop_data,\n                   ...[truncated, total 726 chars]","new_string":"build_autoident_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, auto_data, pop_data,\n                   ...[truncated, total 818 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -476,17 +476,19 @@\n build_autoident_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, auto_data, pop_data,\n                                           caption = \"Ethnic identification (2024)\",\n                                           source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n-  row      <- auto_data[auto_data$ine_code == id_muni, ]\n+  row      <- auto_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n+                                          nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n+              dplyr::slice(1)\n   pop_2024 <- pop_data$pop_2024[pop_data$id_muni == id_muni]\n \n   meta_cols <- c(\"ine_code\", \"municipio\", \"provincia\", \"departamento\",\n                  \"area\", \"nivel\", \"total\",\n-                 # English variants\n                  \"municipality\", \"province\", \"department\", \"level\")\n \n+  grp_cols <- names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols]\n   groups <- tibble::tibble(\n-    group = names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols],\n-    n     = as.numeric(row[names(row)[!names(row) %in% meta_cols]])\n+    group = grp_cols,\n+    n     = sapply(grp_cols, function(col) as.double(row[[col]]))\n   ) |>\n     dplyr::mutate(pct = n / pop_2024) |>\n     dplyr::filter(pct >= 0.01) |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

Both tables work correctly. A few things to note from the output:

- The Aymara row in Sucre's language table shows 0.8% in 2024 but 1.2% in 2012, so it correctly appears (filter is "≥1% in either year")
- The 1% threshold is correctly applied — Jalq'a (1.0%) just makes the cut for the identity table

Summary of all changes:

**`src/wikipedia-tools.R`** — four new functions:
- `build_language_wikitable_en` / `build_language_kable_en` — native language columns (non-`_ext_`), both years, % of total population, rows ≥1% in either year
- `build_autoident_wikitable_en` / `build_autoident_kable_en` — 2024 only, % of total population, rows ≥1%
- All four use `nivel == "Municipio/TIOC"` filter and `dplyr::filter()` for safe NA handling
- `.clean_lang_name()`, `.fmt_n()`, `.fmt_pct()`, `.append_source_row()` internal helpers

**`bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd` — setup block:**
- `pop_muni` now also selects `pop_2012 = pop_2012_total`
- Loads `lang_data` and `auto_data` from `data/cpv2024/`

**`bolivia-muni-generator-en.qmd` — `muni_block`:**
- Two new side-by-side blocks added above the council table: ethnic identification and language, each with kable preview on the left and wikitext copy button on the right

Municipality #89 in `muni_lookup` has an `id_muni` not present in `auto_data`, so `dplyr::filter` returns a 0-row tibble. `row[[col]]` gives `numeric(0)`, and `sapply` over those returns a **list** instead of a vector — which then hits "non-numeric argument" when divided. The wikitable functions already have an `nrow == 0` guard; the kable functions don't. Quick fix:

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01JBeCMqQchgTogFcFsDHoZ1"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"build_language_kable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data) {\n  row      <- lang_dat...[truncated, total 376 chars]","new_string":"build_language_kable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data) {\n  row <- lang_data |> ...[truncated, total 465 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -514,10 +514,12 @@\n \n #' HTML kable preview of language use\n build_language_kable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data) {\n-  row      <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n-                                          nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n-              dplyr::slice(1)\n+  row <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n+                                     nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n+         dplyr::slice(1)\n+  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(htmltools::HTML(\"\"))\n   pop_row  <- pop_data |> dplyr::filter(id_muni == .env$id_muni) |> dplyr::slice(1)\n+  if (nrow(pop_row) == 0) return(htmltools::HTML(\"\"))\n   pop_2024 <- pop_row$pop_2024; pop_2012 <- pop_row$pop_2012\n \n   cols_2024 <- names(lang_data) |>\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_018s79mCVPBQNDZgwhqHXQdK"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"name":"edit","input":{"file_path":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","old_string":"  row      <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n                            ...[truncated, total 388 chars]","new_string":"  row <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n                                 ...[truncated, total 443 chars]"},"caption":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","captionLink":"src/wikipedia-tools.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"@@ -416,10 +416,12 @@\n build_language_wikitable_en <- function(id_muni, lang_data, pop_data,\n                                          caption = \"Languages spoken\",\n                                          source = NULL, source_refs = NULL) {\n-  row      <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n-                                          nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n-              dplyr::slice(1)\n+  row <- lang_data |> dplyr::filter(ine_code == id_muni,\n+                                     nivel == \"Municipio/TIOC\") |>\n+         dplyr::slice(1)\n+  if (nrow(row) == 0) return(\"\")\n   pop_row  <- pop_data |> dplyr::filter(id_muni == .env$id_muni) |> dplyr::slice(1)\n+  if (nrow(pop_row) == 0) return(\"\")\n   pop_2024 <- pop_row$pop_2024\n   pop_2012 <- pop_row$pop_2012\n \n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

All four functions now return an empty result early when either the demographic data or the population data has no row for the given `id_muni`, preventing the `sapply`-into-list coercion entirely.
